## btvmadness-adapt-report-deepseek-1.md
> **LLM attribution:** DeepSeek-V3 - report 1

```markdown
# Tiwas TTRPG Systems Readiness Audit

## Beyond the Vale of Madness (GURPS) as Development Benchmark

**Document ID:** TIWAS-AUDIT-001  
**Date:** 2026-09-01  
**Status:** Formal Systems Readiness Report  
**Auditor:** Tiwas Systems Readiness Auditor

---

## 1. Executive Summary

This report audits the readiness of Tiwas TTRPG to support the published adventure **"Beyond the Vale of Madness"** (GURPS edition), using it as an external benchmark to identify development gaps and prioritize subsystem completion.

### Key Findings

**Overall Readiness: 11 of 19 mechanical systems (57.9%) are sufficiently established for playtesting.**

### Critical Blockers to Full Playtesting

| Priority | Blocker | Responsible System |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | No NPC/monster stat blocks exist | S-12 (Creature Content) — Design-stage only |
| 2 | No treasure/economy framework | Economy subsystem — Missing |
| 3 | No magic subsystem | Magic subsystem — Missing (can avoid) |

### Verdict

**The adventure is partially playtestable, not fully.** It can be played through with provisional stat blocks and economy hand-waving, but full Tiwas-native playtesting requires completion of S-12 creature stat blocks and an economy framework. The core resolution mechanics, combat resolution, injury/wound systems, resource management, healing, progression, and opposed contest systems are fully operational.

---

## 2. Audit Objective and Method

### 2.1 Objective

To use the published **Beyond the Vale of Madness — GURPS** adventure as an external practical benchmark for Tiwas TTRPG by:

1. Inventorying every game-mechanical system the adventure actually uses or assumes
2. Assessing each against current Tiwas capabilities
3. Identifying which unresolved Tiwas systems prevent full playtesting
4. Deriving development priorities from the analysis

### 2.2 Method

| Step | Description |
|---|---|
| 1 | Analyse the adventure PDF to extract all mechanical systems required |
| 2 | Distinguish genuine Tiwas system requirements from adventure content |
| 3 | Map each requirement to Tiwas subsystem(s) |
| 4 | Assess Tiwas capability against established baseline |
| 5 | Classify readiness using defined categories |
| 6 | Identify blockers and development priorities |

### 2.3 Tiwas Baseline Used

| Source | Status | Scope |
|---|---|---|
| Canonical Core (DEC-001–016) | Canonical / Locked | Core Test, Attributes, Skills, XP, Recovery |
| S-1 Opposed Contest (DEC-013) | Canonical / Locked | Universal opposed contest primitive |
| S-2 Zero-Step (DEC-014/037/039) | Canonical / Locked + Ruled | Location Index generation |
| S-3 Effect Menu (DEC-023–030/031) | Non-canonical designer ruling | Effect structure and gating |
| S-4 Wound/Injury (DEC-032–037/041/042) | Non-canonical designer ruling | Injury, Wounds, anatomical mapping |
| S-5 Armor (DEC-058–062) | Non-canonical designer ruling | Tags/Traits armor system |
| S-6 Defense (DEC-044–050) | Non-canonical designer ruling | Active Defense mitigation |
| S-7 Incapacitation (DEC-052–057) | Non-canonical designer ruling | HP=0 incapacitation, death |
| S-8 Difficulty (DEC-063–066) | Non-canonical designer ruling | Difficulty grades, adjudication |
| S-9/S-10 Extended Tests (DEC-067–070) | Non-canonical designer ruling | Margin-accumulation extended tests |
| S-11 Rest/Healing (DEC-071–074) | Non-canonical designer ruling | Healing as Extended Test |
| S-12 Creature Content (DEC-076/077) | Non-canonical designer ruling | **Design-stage only — no stat blocks** |
| Economy subsystem | None | **Missing** |
| Magic subsystem | None | **Missing** |

---

## 3. Source Adventure Mechanical Inventory

### 3.1 Adventure Overview

**Beyond the Vale of Madness** is a solo adventure where a single character:

1. Treks through a frozen wilderness to reach a legendary castle
2. Explores the castle through branching paths
3. Encounters monsters (Ice Troll, Blood Man, Tundra Wolves)
4. Finds treasure and equipment
5. Interacts with NPCs (injured hunter)
6. Returns to town to sell loot and gain advancement

### 3.2 Mechanical System Inventory

The adventure uses or assumes the following game-mechanical systems:

| # | Mechanical System | Adventure Usage | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Skill Rolls | Primary resolution for all character actions | Constant |
| 2 | Attribute Checks | Force doors, climbing, perception, Will saves | Frequent |
| 3 | Combat Resolution | Troll fight, Blood Man fight | 2 major encounters |
| 4 | Damage & Injury | Combat damage, falls, environmental hazards | Frequent |
| 5 | Resource Management | Fatigue Points from cold/exertion | Several points |
| 6 | Equipment | Weapons, armor, gear with stats | Constant |
| 7 | NPC/Monster Stats | Ice Troll, Blood Man, Tundra Wolves | 3 required entries |
| 8 | Environmental Hazards | Cold damage, hypothermia, Survival rolls | Multiple |
| 9 | Healing/Recovery | First Aid, Physician, bandaging | 1 required instance |
| 10 | Progression/XP | Character advancement post-adventure | End-of-adventure |
| 11 | Treasure/Economy | Loot values, selling items | End-of-adventure |
| 12 | Lockpicking/Forced Entry | Guardhouse door obstacle | 1 required instance |
| 13 | Social Interaction | Injured hunter encounter | 1 required instance |
| 14 | Stealth/Sneaking | Bypassing Ice Troll | 1 optional instance |
| 15 | Climbing | Scaling castle walls | 1 optional instance |
| 16 | Perception/Search | Finding treasure, noticing details | Multiple |
| 17 | Fear/Morale | Fright Checks (Blood Man, corpse discovery) | 2 required instances |
| 18 | Tracking | Navigating icy maze | 1 optional instance |
| 19 | Merchant/Trade | Appraising and selling treasure | End-of-adventure |
| 20 | Magic/Spells | Referenced for advanced character option | Optional (can avoid) |

---

## 4. System-by-System Tiwas Compatibility Assessment

### 4.1 Core Resolution Systems

#### 4.1.1 Skill Rolls

| Attribute | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Adventure Requirement** | GURPS 3d6 roll-under for all skill-based actions |
| **Adventure Usage** | Climbing, Stealth, Tracking, First Aid, Lockpicking, Forced Entry, Survival, etc. |
| **Tiwas Capability** | Core Test (DEC-006) — d100 roll-under with full 9-step transaction |
| **Authority/Status** | DEC-001–016 Canonical / Locked |
| **Readiness Classification** | ✅ **Playtestable Now** |
| **Blocking Significance** | None — full operational capability |
| **Remaining Work** | None; complete |

**Mapping:** All GURPS skill rolls become ordinary Tiwas Core Tests. Success = `Roll <= Effective Skill`. Roll of 100 always fails. Cost = natural roll paid from Physical Energy or MP. Overflow→HP if insufficient resource. Failure XP = max(0, Roll - Skill). Skill Roll Pool advancement. Recovery after every test.

---

#### 4.1.2 Attribute Checks

| Attribute | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Adventure Requirement** | ST checks (force doors), DX checks (balance/climbing), Will rolls (fear), PER rolls (search) |
| **Adventure Usage** | Breaking doors, climbing, Fright Checks, searching, etc. |
| **Tiwas Capability** | 24-Attribute Matrix (DEC-003) with derived stats; Core Test using appropriate Body/Mind attribute as Skill |
| **Authority/Status** | DEC-003 Canonical / Locked |
| **Readiness Classification** | ✅ **Playtestable Now** |
| **Blocking Significance** | None |
| **Remaining Work** | Attribute→Skill mapping for GURPS attributes (see Adaptation Mapping section) |

**Mapping:** GURPS ST → relevant Body Power attributes (Bear/Tiger/Elephant). GURPS DX → Body Speed attributes (Hawk/Rat/Horse). GURPS IQ → Mind Power attributes (Stag/Snake/Rooster). GURPS HT → Body Endurance (Badger/Wolf/Ox). All checks use Core Test mechanics.

---

### 4.2 Combat and Injury Systems

#### 4.2.1 Combat Resolution

| Attribute | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Adventure Requirement** | Attack vs Defense, damage rolls, tactical positioning, enemy tactics |
| **Adventure Usage** | Ice Troll fight (#17), Blood Man fight (#38) |
| **Tiwas Capability** | S-1 Opposed Contest (DEC-013) + S-3 Effects (DEC-023–030) + S-6 Defense (DEC-044–050) |
| **Authority/Status** | DEC-013 Canonical / Locked; DEC-023–030/044–050 Non-canonical designer rulings |
| **Readiness Classification** | ✅ **Playtestable Now** (with stat blocks) |
| **Blocking Significance** | Combat mechanics are complete; stat blocks are the blocker |
| **Remaining Work** | S-12 stat blocks for monsters (see Section 6.1) |

**Mapping:** Each combat exchange = S-1 Opposed Contest between attacker's relevant Skill and defender's defense Skill. Winner declares Effect per S-3 menu (Base: Inflict Injury, Impose Condition). Effect auto-applies (DEC-027). Armor Tags checked (S-5). Active Defense mitigation per S-6. Quality determines Effect tier availability (DEC-031). Separate Defense roll per Effect (DEC-049). Tactical positioning and enemy tactics handled by GM discretion.

---

#### 4.2.2 Damage and Injury

| Attribute | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Adventure Requirement** | HP damage from weapons, falls (1d-1, 2d-2), environmental cold damage |
| **Adventure Usage** | Combat, falling, cold exposure |
| **Tiwas Capability** | Overflow→HP (DEC-007) + S-4 Wound/Injury (DEC-032–037/041/042) |
| **Authority/Status** | DEC-007 Canonical / Locked; DEC-032–037/041/042 Non-canonical designer rulings |
| **Readiness Classification** | ✅ **Playtestable Now** |
| **Blocking Significance** | None — damage/injury systems are operational |
| **Remaining Work** | Damage magnitude scaling (see Adaptation Mapping) |

**Mapping:** GURPS damage dice convert to Tiwas Injury Effects with GM-adjudicated magnitudes. Overflow from insufficient PE/MP becomes direct HP damage. Wounds inflicted via "Impose Condition: Wounded" Effect requiring Skill-Tier ≥2 and Location Index. HP=0 triggers incapacitation (DEC-052). Falls: non-attack physical consequence uses DEC-037 governing failed roll for Location Index.

---

#### 4.2.3 Fear/Morale (Fright Checks)

| Attribute | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Adventure Requirement** | Fright Check (p. B360) — Blood Man appearance, corpse discovery |
| **Adventure Usage** | #38 (Blood Man), #58 (half-eaten corpse) |
| **Tiwas Capability** | Core Test using appropriate Mind Skill (Resolve/Composure/Willpower) + Impose Condition: Shaken/Frightened (Condition tier) |
| **Authority/Status** | Core Test: Canonical / Locked; Condition Effects: S-3 menu structure (DEC-023) — contents not fully enumerated |
| **Readiness Classification** | 🔄 **Adaptation Mapping Required** |
| **Blocking Significance** | Low — can be substituted via GM discretion using Core Test + Condition |
| **Remaining Work** | Enumerate Condition-tier Effects; formal Fright Check mechanic optional |

**Adaptation Note:** No formal Fright Check subsystem exists. This can be adapted as a Willpower Core Test (Mind attribute, Resolve/Composure) with the successful Effect being "Impose Condition: Frightened" (or similar). The Condition tier Effects are not yet fully enumerated (see Part C of the corpus).

---

### 4.3 Resource and Recovery Systems

#### 4.3.1 Resource Management (Fatigue Points)

| Attribute | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Adventure Requirement** | Fatigue Points (FP) from cold exposure, exertion; subtract 1 FP for digging, cold treks |
| **Adventure Usage** | #10 (digging: subtract 1 FP), #19 (cold: reduce FP by 1), #21 (pounding ice: costs 2 FP) |
| **Tiwas Capability** | Physical Energy (bep + bes + bee) + Energy Regen (bep + bes); Core Test costs paid from PE; Overflow→HP |
| **Authority/Status** | DEC-004/007/008 Canonical / Locked |
| **Readiness Classification** | ✅ **Playtestable Now** |
| **Blocking Significance** | None |
| **Remaining Work** | None |

**Mapping:** GURPS FP maps directly to Tiwas Physical Energy. Exertion costs in the adventure (digging, cold, pounding ice) are simple PE deductions (not full Core Tests) handled via resource tracking. PE recovery after each test per DEC-008. If PE reaches 0, further costs cause Overflow→HP damage.

---

#### 4.3.2 Healing and Recovery

| Attribute | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Adventure Requirement** | First Aid/Physician rolls to restore HP; resting |
| **Adventure Usage** | #21 (First Aid restore 1d-3 HP) |
| **Tiwas Capability** | S-11 Rest/Healing (DEC-071–074) — Extended Test instance |
| **Authority/Status** | DEC-071–074 Non-canonical designer rulings |
| **Readiness Classification** | ✅ **Playtestable Now** |
| **Blocking Significance** | None |
| **Remaining Work** | Wound magnitude→HP restoration mapping (see Adaptation Mapping) |

**Mapping:** Each rest period = Extended Test interval (DEC-073). Healer's effective Skill penalized by wound magnitude (DEC-072). Success adds Margin (Skill - Roll) to progress. Failures neutral. Completion target = GM discretion (DEC-074). The GURPS "restore 1d-3 HP" becomes "reduce wound magnitude by Margin amount" with GM-adjudicated mapping.

---

### 4.4 Equipment and Economy

#### 4.4.1 Equipment (Weapons, Armor, Gear)

| Attribute | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Adventure Requirement** | Weapons with damage values (mace: sw+2 cr), armor DR, shield stats, equipment weight |
| **Adventure Usage** | Throughout; combat stats, encumbrance, item values |
| **Tiwas Capability** | S-5 Armor (DEC-058–062) — Tags/Traits system; weapon damage as S-3 Injury Effects |
| **Authority/Status** | DEC-058–062 Non-canonical designer rulings |
| **Readiness Classification** | 🔄 **Adaptation Mapping Required** |
| **Blocking Significance** | Low — can improvise weapon damage Tags; armor Tags operational |
| **Remaining Work** | Weapon stat table (damage categories, Tags); encumbrance system (missing) |

**Adaptation Note:** Tiwas armor uses Tags/Traits rather than numeric DR. Armor coverage location-bound; Bypass as Tag-pair matching. Weapons lack formal stat tables but can be represented by Tags defining the Injury Effect magnitude (e.g., "Heavy Blunt: Injury Effect +2"). Encumbrance is not defined in Tiwas.

---

#### 4.4.2 Treasure and Economy

| Attribute | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Adventure Requirement** | Treasure values ($50, $300, etc.), selling loot, merchant rolls |
| **Adventure Usage** | End-of-adventure (#60) — selling wares, receiving rewards |
| **Tiwas Capability** | None — no currency framework, no valuation system, no merchant procedure |
| **Authority/Status** | ❌ **Missing Subsystem** |
| **Blocking Significance** | **High** — required for complete adventure resolution |
| **Remaining Work** | Define currency system, item valuation framework, merchant/appraisal procedure |

**Critical Gap:** The adventure's conclusion depends on treasure valuation, selling, and economic reward. Tiwas has no economy subsystem to support this. While the adventure can be played through without it (stop at the castle exit), full playtesting requires a functional economy.

#### 4.4.3 Magic/Spells

| Attribute | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Adventure Requirement** | Referenced for advanced character option ("uses spells from GURPS Magic") |
| **Adventure Usage** | Optional pregenerated character only |
| **Tiwas Capability** | None — no magic subsystem |
| **Authority/Status** | ❌ **Missing Subsystem** |
| **Blocking Significance** | **Low** — can use non-magical pregenerated characters |
| **Remaining Work** | Magic subsystem design (deferrable) |

**Note:** The adventure does not require magic to be playable. The "advanced character" pregen is optional. This is a soft blocker only.

### 4.5 Environmental and Exploration Systems

#### 4.5.1 Environmental Hazards

| Attribute | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Adventure Requirement** | Cold damage, hypothermia, Survival (Mountains) rolls, exhausting conditions |
| **Adventure Usage** | #19 (cold winds: Survival roll, 1d-3 damage), #10 (digging costs FP) |
| **Tiwas Capability** | Core Test + Difficulty grades (DEC-063–066) + Non-attack LI (DEC-037) |
| **Authority/Status** | Core Test: Canonical / Locked; DEC-063–066/037: Non-canonical designer rulings |
| **Readiness Classification** | 🔄 **Adaptation Mapping Required** |
| **Blocking Significance** | Low — can substitute via Core Test + GM adjudication |
| **Remaining Work** | Formal environment hazard procedure (optional) |

**Adaptation Note:** Survival roll = Core Test using appropriate Body/Mind Skill (Survival/Tracking/Quickness). Difficulty modifiers via DEC-063 named tiers. Cold damage = Overflow/Injury Effects. Non-attack Location Index via DEC-037 for injury location if needed.

#### 4.5.2 Climbing

|---|---|

| **Adventure Usage** | #15 (climb icy cliff), #35 (climb castle wall) |

| **Authority/Status** | Core Test: Canonical / Locked |
| **Readiness Classification** | ✅ **Playtestable Now** |
| **Blocking Significance** | None |
| **Remaining Work** | None |

**Mapping:** Climbing = Core Test with difficulty modifiers (icy = Hard difficulty, DEC-063). Failure → fall → Injury Effect with Location Index from non-attack roll (DEC-037). Success → continue.

#### 4.5.3 Stealth/Sneaking

| **Adventure Usage** | #44 (sneak past Ice Troll: Stealth vs Perception 12 Quick Contest) |

| **Authority/Status** | DEC-013 Canonical / Locked |
| **Readiness Classification** | ✅ **Playtestable Now** |
| **Blocking Significance** | None |
| **Remaining Work** | None |

**Mapping:** Stealth vs Perception = S-1 Opposed Contest. Each participant's Core Test independent. Success vs Success → compare Quality (Margin or Blackjack). Failure vs Failure → repeat. Winner determines Effect (sneak past vs detection).

## 6. Missing / Design-Stage Dependencies

### 6.1 Critical Blocker: S-12 Creature Content

**Current Status:** Design-stage only (DEC-076/077)

**Decisions Made:**
- Dual-mode fork: computerized systems use full 24-attribute generation; tabletop uses abbreviated stat-block method
- Content authoring path: actual creature instances developed via playtesting, not drafted by advisory models

**Remaining Work Required:**
- Ice Troll stat block:
  - 24-Attribute Matrix values (Body/Mind)
  - Relevant Skills with Tiers/Caps
  - S-1/S-3/S-6 integration
  - Wound/Overflow handling
  - Resource pools (PE/MP)
  - Enemy tactics (per GURPS stat block: All-Out Attacks at ≤0 HP)

- Blood Man stat block:
  - 24-Attribute Matrix values
  - Skills (Brawling, Camouflage, Stealth)
  - Blood Seep ability (grapple → corrosive damage)
  - S-1/S-3/S-6 integration

- Tundra Wolves stat block:
  - 24-Attribute Matrix values
  - Skills (Stealth, Tracking)
  - Pack tactics (implicit)

**Impact:** Cannot run the adventure's key encounters without these stat blocks.

---

### 6.2 High Blocker: Treasure/Economy

**Current Status:** Missing

**Requirements:**
- Currency framework (GURPS $ → Tiwas currency)
- Item valuation system (based on treasure table)
- Merchant/appraisal procedure
- Loot disposition mechanics

**Treasure Items Requiring Valuation:**

| Silver tiara | $300 | [Undefined] |

**Impact:** Cannot resolve the post-adventure phase (#60) without economy framework.

## 9. Development Priorities Exposed by the Adventure

### 9.1 Critical Priorities (Block Playtesting)

| Priority | System | Required Work | Estimated Effort |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **S-12 Creature Stat Blocks** | Create Ice Troll, Blood Man, Tundra Wolves stat blocks with Tiwas attributes, Skills, and combat integration | Medium |
| 2 | **Economy Subsystem** | Define currency framework; item valuation; merchant/appraisal procedure | Small-Medium |

**Why these are critical:**
- **S-12 stat blocks:** Without monsters, the adventure's two major combat encounters cannot be resolved. The adventure is primarily a combat/exploration module; combat is essential.
- **Economy:** Without an economy, the conclusion (#60) cannot be resolved. The adventure has a defined end-state requiring treasure valuation.

### 9.2 High Priorities (Complete the Experience)

| Priority | System | Required Work | Estimated Effort |
|---|---|---|---|
| 3 | **Fear/Morale subsystem** | Enumerate Condition-tier S-3 Effects; formalize Fright Check as Core Test + Condition | Small |
| 4 | **Weapon stat mapping** | Define weapon Tags (Damage Effect magnitude, Reach, etc.) | Small |
| 5 | **Encumbrance system** | Define encumbrance mechanics | Small |

### 9.3 Medium Priorities (Quality of Life)

| Priority | System | Required Work | Estimated Effort |
|---|---|---|---|
| 6 | **Environmental hazard procedure** | Formalize hazard difficulty, cold damage scaling | Small |
| 7 | **Social interaction/reaction** | Formalize reaction/relationship subsystem (optional for this adventure) | Medium |

```
Phase 1 (Immediate)
    ├── 1. Create Ice Troll stat block (enables main combat)
    ├── 2. Create Blood Man stat block (enables secondary combat)
    └── 3. Create Tundra Wolves stat block (enables environmental threat)

Phase 2 (Before Playtest)
    ├── 4. Define currency framework
    ├── 5. Define item valuation system
    └── 6. Define merchant/appraisal procedure

Phase 3 (Playtest)

Phase 4 (Post-Playtest, Iterative)

| Category | Count | Percentage |
|---|---|---|
| ✅ Playtestable Now | 11 | 57.9% |
| 🔄 Adaptation Mapping Required | 4 | 21.1% |
| ⚠️ Design-Stage Dependency | 1 | 5.3% |
| ❌ Missing Subsystem | 3 | 15.8% |

### 10.2 Key Verdict

**The adventure is NOT fully playtestable in Tiwas as-is. Two critical blockers prevent complete playtesting:**

1. **No NPC/monster stat blocks (S-12)** — The Ice Troll, Blood Man, and Tundra Wolves cannot be run in Tiwas without stat blocks. This is a **Design-Stage Dependency**.

2. **No treasure/economy framework** — The post-adventure phase (#60) requires currency, valuation, and merchant mechanics. This is a **Missing Subsystem**.

### 10.3 What Is Playtestable Now

The following systems can be tested using this adventure (with provisional stat blocks and economy hand-waving):

- ✅ Core Test Transaction (DEC-006)
- ✅ 24-Attribute Matrix (DEC-003)
- ✅ Resource Management (DEC-004/007/008)
- ✅ S-1 Opposed Contest (DEC-013)
- ✅ S-3 Effect Menu (DEC-023–030)
- ✅ S-4 Wound/Injury (DEC-032–037/041/042)
- ✅ S-6 Active Defense (DEC-044–050)
- ✅ S-7 Incapacitation (DEC-052–057)
- ✅ S-8 Difficulty Grades (DEC-063–066)
- ✅ S-9/S-10 Extended Tests (DEC-067–070)
- ✅ S-11 Healing (DEC-071–074)
- ✅ Progression/XP (DEC-009–011)
- ✅ Exploration skills (Climbing, Stealth, Tracking, Perception)

### 10.4 Recommended Next Actions

| Action | Owner | Timeline |
|---|---|---|
| 1. Create Ice Troll stat block | Development | Immediate |
| 2. Create Blood Man stat block | Development | Immediate |
| 3. Create Tundra Wolves stat block | Development | Immediate |
| 4. Define Tiwas currency framework | Development | Before playtest |
| 5. Define item valuation system | Development | Before playtest |
| 6. Run playtest with complete systems | Playtest | After items 1-5 |

### 10.5 Broader Implications for Tiwas Development

This audit reveals that Tiwas has achieved **functional completeness** for core resolution, combat, injury, resource management, healing, and progression. The remaining gaps are:

1. **Content authoring (S-12):** Stat blocks are not system mechanics per se, but content. However, the adventure cannot be played without them. This suggests S-12 content authoring is a higher priority than previously assumed.

2. **Economy:** The absence of an economy subsystem is the second major blocker. This is a genuine missing subsystem that affects all treasure-based adventures.

3. **Magic:** Can be deferred but will be required for fantasy adventures beyond this one.

**The audit successfully identified these gaps using a published adventure as an objective benchmark, confirming its value as a development diagnostic tool.**

---
