```markdown
---

## 6. MISSING / DESIGN-STAGE DEPENDENCIES

### 6.1 Critical Blockers (Adventure Cannot Run Without These)

**A. S-3 Base-Tier Damage Quantification (Design-Stage)**
The S-3 Effect menu (DEC-023) defines *Inflict Injury* as "HP-only" but provides no mechanism for determining the quantity of HP damage. The adventure expects 1d+2, 1d, 2d-2, etc. Possible resolutions within existing Tiwas architecture:
- Damage = attacker's Margin of Success (Skill − Roll)
- Damage = fixed value per weapon/attack type
- Damage = separate die roll (violates Invariant 6 if Cost-based; introduces new mechanic)
- Damage = natural roll of the attacker's Core Test (already paid as Cost; would double-spend the roll)

**Status:** No ruling exists. The designer must specify how *Inflict Injury* quantifies HP removal.

**B. Skill Defaults / Untrained Use (Design-Stage)**
Eight adventure calls require attempting a skill without training. Tiwas has no default mechanism. A Skill-0 test can never succeed (d100 range 1–100, success requires Roll ≤ 0). Options:
- Untrained = Skill 0, always fails (adventure becomes impossible on those paths)
- Untrained = effective Skill at some fraction of governing attribute(s)
- Untrained = separate "default" skill with its own Cap formula

**Status:** No ruling exists. The designer must specify whether and how untrained skills are attempted.

**C. S-6 Defense Skill Specification (Design-Stage)**
S-6 Active Defense (DEC-044–050) rules that the defender makes a genuine Core Test, but does not specify which skill or attribute. The adventure's "Dodge 9" and "Parry 10" need Tiwas equivalents. Options:
- Defense skill = Reflexes (bss) or Grace (bsx) for Dodge
- Defense skill = Shield skill or a new Parry skill
- Defense = attribute test (e.g., Speed-derived)

**Status:** No ruling exists. The designer must specify the defense skill/attribute.

### 6.2 High-Priority Dependencies (Resolve for Full Experience)

**D. Trait Framework (Missing)**
Thirty-four GURPS traits have no Tiwas equivalent. This blocks pregen construction. Some traits are purely narrative (Loves reading adventure books); others are mechanically load-bearing (Magery 3, Luck, Combat Reflexes, Night Vision, Dwarf racial package). A general Tag/Trait framework beyond S-5's armor Tags is needed.

**E. Magic Subsystem (Missing)**
Nineteen spells with energy costs, ranges, durations. Tiwas has no magic implementation. The dwarf pregen allows play without magic, but this is a degraded experience.

**F. S-4 Wound Consequences (OPEN-007, Not Table-Ready)**
Wound creation is Ruled, but mechanical consequences (attribute penalties, healing cost scaling) are not. The Blood Man's persistent corrosive damage implies a wound-like condition that would benefit from this.

### 6.3 Medium-Priority Dependencies

**G. Damage Type Distinctions (Missing)**
cut/cr/imp/cor have no mechanical meaning. Armor penetration logic (soft armor vs. falls, DR vs. specific types) cannot be replicated.

**H. Encumbrance (Missing)**
One adventure call references encumbrance level penalizing Climbing. Tiwas has no encumbrance system.

**I. Weapon Stat Framework (Missing)**
Reach, ST requirement, weight. The mace "requires ST 12" has no Tiwas equivalent.

**J. Tactical Combat (Missing)**
Battle maps exist but are optional. "Trading blows" is a valid alternative.

---

## 7. ADVENTURE PATH DEPENDENCY ANALYSIS

The adventure is a branching solo module. Not all mechanics are required on all paths.

### 7.1 Path Tracing Summary

| Path Segment | Mechanics Required | Avoidable? |
|--------------|-------------------|------------|
| Entry: Cave (#12) vs. Surface (#19) | Cave: Stealth, troll combat. Surface: Survival, cold exposure. | Yes (choose surface) |
| Cave branches: #9 (trodden) vs. #22 (icier) vs. #54 (warmer) | #9: Troll combat mandatory if taken. #22/#54: Avoid troll. | Yes (choose #22/#54) |
| Courtyard (#3): Search, two doors | Search roll optional. Doors lead to same interior. | Partial |
| Guardhouse (#11): Lockpicking or Forced Entry | Both require skill tests. | No (must enter castle) |
| Servant's entrance (#14): Search/Perception | Optional. | Yes |
| Banquet hall (#33): Hole or tapestries | Both lead to blood man. | No (must proceed) |
| Blood Man chamber (#38): Combat or flee | Combat mandatory for full adventure. Flee → early ending (#53→#60). | Yes (but ends adventure) |
| Troll lair (#17): Combat or flee | Combat mandatory for cave path. Flee → troll pursues. | Partial |

### 7.2 Minimum-Viable Path (No Combat)

Surface entry (#19) → Courtyard (#3) → Servant's door (#14) → Banquet hall (#33) → Hole (#55) → Flee (#53) → Epilogue (#60).

**Mechanics on this path:** Survival (Mountains), DX (stairs), Search/Perception (optional), First Aid (optional), Merchant (optional), DX (hole jump), Will (scepter room, optional).

**Tiwas readiness for this path:** All mechanics are Playtestable Now except:
- Survival default (if untrained) → Design-Stage
- DX "default" → not applicable (DX is an attribute, not a skill)
- Healing amounts → Adaptation Mapping

**Verdict:** This path is **runnable today** with minimal GM adjudication. However, it comprises approximately 15% of the adventure's content and avoids both combat encounters and most treasures.

### 7.3 Full-Adventure Path (All Content)

Any path that collects significant treasure and defeats both monsters requires:
- Both combat encounters (troll and blood man)
- All skill tests including defaults
- All healing calls
- Tactical combat (optional but intended)
- Magic (if using Enfys)

**Verdict:** The full adventure is **not runnable** without items 1–3 from the critical path (damage quantification, skill defaults, defense skill spec).

---

## 8. TIWAS READINESS ASSESSMENT

### 8.1 What Tiwas Does Well
The core engine is robust and fully functional. The recent rulings on S-6 through S-11 demonstrate rapid subsystem maturation. The 24-attribute matrix provides granular character differentiation. The S-1/S-3/S-6/S-7 combat pipeline (contest → Effect → defense → incapacitation) is architecturally complete; it lacks only damage quantification and defense skill specification to become operational.

### 8.2 What Blocks Playtest
Three gaps prevent even a degraded playtest of the full adventure:
1. **Combat damage has no quantity.** The S-3 base tier is qualitatively defined ("HP-only") but quantitatively empty.
2. **Untrained characters cannot succeed.** Eight adventure paths expect non-zero untrained success chance; Tiwas gives 0%.
3. **Defense rolls have no skill.** S-6 is architecturally complete but mechanically unspecified.

### 8.3 What Degrades Experience
Five missing subsystems prevent full fidelity but do not block play:
- Traits (pregens are generic)
- Magic (Enfys is unplayable; dwarf-only runs are possible)
- Tactical combat (maps are unused)
- Damage types (armor is simplified)
- Encumbrance (climbing penalty is ignored)

---

## 9. DEVELOPMENT PRIORITIES EXPOSED BY THE ADVENTURE

### 9.1 Immediate (Pre-First-Session)
1. **Rule: S-3 Base-Tier Damage Quantification** — Specify how *Inflict Injury* determines HP loss. Recommendation: test Margin-of-Success = damage as the default (preserves existing roll, no new dice, rewards high skill).
2. **Rule: Skill Defaults** — Specify untrained attempt mechanics. Recommendation: untrained effective Skill = floor(governing attribute / 2), or a fixed penalty schedule (e.g., −20 to effective Skill per missing Tier).
3. **Rule: S-6 Defense Skill** — Designate which skill/attribute powers Active Defense. Recommendation: Reflexes (bss) for Dodge; Shield skill or Broadsword/Axe skill for Parry.

### 9.2 Short-Term (Pre-Full-Playtest)
4. **Draft: Trait/Tag Framework** — Generalize S-5's armor Tags into a character trait system. Start with the 10–12 most common traits (racial packages, sensory traits, combat traits).
5. **Draft: Magic Subsystem** — Define spellcasting as Mind-skill Extended Tests with MP costs, or as a separate resource economy (requires Invariant 17 assessment).
6. **Draft: S-4 Wound Consequences** — Resolve OPEN-007: attribute penalties per wound magnitude, healing cost scaling.

### 9.3 Medium-Term
7. **Draft: Damage Type System** — Define cut/cr/imp/cor as Tags that interact with armor Tags (S-5 Bypass extension).
8. **Draft: Encumbrance and Weapon Stats** — Weight, reach, ST requirements. Encumbrance could be a Condition-tier Effect or a derived Movement Speed modifier.
9. **Draft: Tactical Combat** — Hex-based positioning, facing, reach. Optional; maps are usable without this.

---

## 10. CONCLUSIONS

**Can we playtest *Beyond the Vale of Madness* in Tiwas now?**  
No — not the full adventure. The minimum-viable no-combat path is runnable with minimal GM adjudication, but this represents approximately 15% of the adventure's content and avoids both mandatory combat encounters.

**Exactly which Tiwas systems must be completed first?**  
Three rulings would unlock a degraded but complete playtest:
1. How much HP does *Inflict Injury* remove?
2. What is the untrained skill default mechanism?
3. Which skill/attribute powers Active Defense?

With these three rulings, the adventure can be played using King Coppertong (no magic), without tactical maps, and with simplified armor. Full fidelity requires additionally: a trait framework, a magic subsystem, and damage type distinctions.

**Is this adventure a good readiness benchmark?**  
Yes. It exercises core resolution, opposed contests, difficulty grades, location indices, incapacitation, healing, and extended tests — all of which are table-ready. It also exposes precisely the gaps (damage quantification, skill defaults, defense specification, traits, magic) that currently prevent full play. It is appropriately scoped for alpha playtesting: complex enough to stress-test the system, simple enough to convert without excessive content overhead.

---

## 11. SOURCE AND EVIDENCE REGISTER

---

## 12. EXPLICIT ASSUMPTIONS / UNRESOLVED QUESTIONS

The following inferences were made where the Tiwas baseline is silent. These are structural necessities, not canonical rules:

1. **GURPS FP maps to Tiwas Physical Energy.** Assumed from functional identity: both are pools depleted by exertion, recovered over time. Tiwas uses Physical Energy (bep+bes+bee) where GURPS uses FP.
2. **GURPS HP maps to Tiwas HP.** Explicitly confirmed by DEC-007 (Overflow→HP damage).
3. **GURPS Speed/Move map to Tiwas Speed/Movement Speed.** Assumed from functional identity; exact numerical conversion is adventure-specific.
4. **GURPS attributes can be mapped to the 24-attribute matrix.** Assumed from structural necessity; no canonical mapping exists. Example mapping (analyst inference only): ST→bpp/bpe, DX→bsp/bss/bse, IQ→mpp/mps/mpe, HT→bep/bes/bee, Will→mpe/mee, Per→mss.
5. **GURPS skills can be expressed as Tiwas Skills with Tier/Cap formulas.** Assumed from structural necessity; specific formulas are adventure-specific content.
6. **GURPS DR can be expressed as S-5 Armor Tags.** Assumed from S-5's Tag-only architecture; specific Tags must be authored.
7. **The adventure's "trading blows" alternative is a valid Tiwas combat mode.** Assumed from the adventure's explicit text and Tiwas's lack of tactical combat; this is a narrative resolution fallback, not a system feature.
8. **S-6 Active Defense can be invoked against S-3 base-tier *Inflict Injury*.** Assumed from DEC-050's universal eligibility ruling; the base-tier Effect is an auto-applied Effect and therefore defensible.

**Unresolved questions for the designer:**
- Should untrained skills use a default formula, and if so, what is it?
- Should *Inflict Injury* damage equal Margin of Success, a fixed weapon value, or something else?
- Which Tiwas skill or attribute should power Active Defense?
- Should magic use MP as its resource (preserving Invariant 17), or introduce a new resource (requiring Invariant 17 exception)?
- Should damage types (cut/cr/imp/cor) be Tags, Conditions, or a new subsystem?

---

*End of Report*
```

> **LLM attribution:** Muse Spark 1.1 - report 1

```markdown
This analysis uses both files you uploaded:

* `Beyond-the-Vale-of-Madness-GURPS.pdf` (19 pages, solo Choose-Your-Path)
* `Tiwas-Alpha-Playtest-Corpus-2026-09-01.md` (one-off GM table-ready compilation dated 2026-09-01)

I have read both in full. Below is the prompt you asked me to draft from your answers, then the report.

### Tailored Prompt

**Role:** TTRPG Systems Analyst and Technical Documentation Specialist – fluent in GURPS Dungeon Fantasy terminology and Tiwas governance (Canonical / Locked vs Non-canonical designer ruling, DEC references, Invariants).

**Context:** I need to adapt the published GURPS solo adventure Beyond the Vale of Madness to Tiwas TTRPG. The purpose is not to publish the conversion, but to measure Tiwas readiness: by forcing a simple, established adventure through Tiwas, we can see which subsystems are already usable and which still need design. Every mechanical system the adventure actually uses or assumes must be inventoried.

**Audience:** Primary: Tiwa (designer) and Tiwas dev team. Secondary: Alpha playtest GMs who need to know what they can run RAW vs what requires GM fiat.

**Format and length:** Formal project documentation in Markdown. Include: Document Control, Executive Summary, Purpose, Methodology, Baseline Definition, Exhaustive Mechanical Inventory of the adventure, Compatibility Analysis mapped to Tiwas corpus, Gap Analysis Matrix, Readiness Metric, Prioritized Recommendations, Risks/Limitations, Appendices. Use tables for the matrix. ~1500-2500 words, no fluff.

**Success criteria:**
1.  Exhaustiveness – every GURPS check, roll modifier, damage, defense, equipment stat, hazard, trait, magic spell, state flag, and progression reward in the module is listed.
2.  Correct governance mapping – each item is judged only against the current Tiwas canonical rules + accepted designer rulings + explicitly accepted non-canonical candidate policies as of 2026-09-01 corpus; everything else is treated as not available. Mechanical equivalence is allowed (e.g., any GURPS skill roll = Tiwas standard skill roll).
3.  Actionable metric – clearly separates Playtestable Now vs Design-Stage Dependency vs Missing Subsystem, and answers: can we playtest this adventure in Tiwas now, and if not, exactly which Tiwas systems must be completed first.

**Constraints:**
- Assess compatibility mechanically, not procedurally. Do not try to preserve GURPS procedures.
- Do not invent Tiwas rules beyond the corpus. If a Tag vocabulary, creature template, or effect content is not in Part A/B, it is not table-ready.
- Do not treat adventure-specific content (story branches, treasure names) as a system gap.
- Flag any inference/guess explicitly.

**Examples:** 
- Reference files: `Beyond-the-Vale-of-Madness-GURPS.pdf` – uses ST roll to dig through snow, DX roll to light torch, Will roll with Fearlessness modifier for Fright Check at -2, Lockpicking at +2 (DX-3 default), Climbing at -1 per encumbrance, Ice Troll stat block ST 15 HP 15 Speed 6.5 DR 2 with Regeneration Fast 1 HP/min only in freezing, etc. 
- Reference baseline: `Tiwas-Alpha-Playtest-Corpus-2026-09-01.md` Part A DEC-001 to DEC-016 (Canonical Locked), Part B DEC-023 to DEC-074 (Ruled non-canonical table-ready), Part C explicitly NOT table-ready including S-12 creature content and OPEN-007 wound consequences.

**Guesses I had to make:**
- You said category is "playtestable now" only – I interpreted this as binary question for final decision, but I retain the four diagnostic categories you originally proposed for transparency. Flagged below.
- I treat GURPS default penalties (DX-5, IQ-4, etc.) as Tiwas effective-skill modifiers via S-8, because Tiwas has no explicit default rule.
- I treat hex tactical map as optional because adventure text says "You can trade blows, or jump into tactical combat using the battle map".
- I treat FP costs (lose 1 FP, 2 FP) as substitution via Tiwas Physical Energy cost + Overflow, not as a separate resource.

---

### PROJECT REPORT – Readiness Assessment: Beyond the Vale of Madness (GURPS) → Tiwas TTRPG

**Document Control**
| Item | Value |
|---|---|
| Title | Tiwas Readiness Assessment via External Adventure Ingest |
| Source Adventure | Beyond the Vale of Madness (GURPS Dungeon Fantasy, v1.2, J.C. Connors) |
| Target System | Tiwas Alpha-Playtest Corpus 2026-09-01 |
| Date | 2026-09-01 |
| Classification | Internal Development Metric |
| Baseline | Part A Canonical/Locked DEC-001–016 + Part B Ruled non-canonical DEC-023–074; Part C = NOT table-ready |
| Author | Systems Analysis Agent |

**Revision History:** v1.0 initial ingest.

#### 1. Executive Summary

Beyond the Vale of Madness was chosen as a minimal viable dungeon: one major combat (Ice Troll), basic exploration, environmental attrition, and two pregens including a spellcaster. Tiwas can already resolve the core loop of this adventure: d100 roll-under skill tests, difficulty-modified effective skill, HP-only injury, overflow exhaustion, opposed contests, Zero-Step location indexing for both attack and non-attack falls, auto-apply effect with Active Defense mitigation, forced incapacitation at HP 0, and Extended Tests for digging/breaking ice.

It cannot yet run the adventure table-ready without GM fiat for: creature stat blocks, equipment/encumbrance/ST requirements, numeric DR soak, magic/Magery/spell costs, specific Armor Tag vocabulary and Bypass pairs, Wound consequence magnitudes, and environmental hazard rates. Magic is a hard blocker if the Enfys Loom pregen is used.

**Verdict:** Partially playtestable. A martial character (King Copperling-style) can be playtested through the non-magical branches with GM decisions for the troll. A full playtest including Enfys Loom, treasure economy, and strict RAW troll regeneration is **not** playtestable until S-12 creature content, equipment/encumbrance, and a magic-exclusion or magic-ruling decision are made.

#### 2. Purpose and Objectives

Measure Tiwas TTRPG development completeness by ingesting an external, simple, published module and mapping every mechanical assumption to the current Tiwas corpus. The resulting metric identifies which subsystems are usable now and which block playtesting.

#### 3. Methodology

1.  Text extraction of all 19 pages of the GURPS module. Inventory of every rule that triggers a die roll, modifier, damage calculation, resource loss, trait check, or state change.
2.  Cross-reference against Tiwas Corpus Parts A–D. Only DEC-001–016 (Locked) and DEC-023–074 (Ruled) counted as available. Part C Open/Not table-ready counted as unavailable.
3.  Mechanical equivalence principle per your instruction: any GURPS skill roll = Tiwas standard skill roll (9-step Core Test Transaction).
4.  Classification per your original taxonomy (retained for diagnostic clarity):

*   **Playtestable Now** – sufficient rules in corpus to run RAW
*   **Playtestable with Substitution** – GURPS mechanism can be replaced by existing Tiwas mechanism without new design
*   **Design-Stage Dependency** – Tiwas has identified/proposed subsystem but not table-ready
*   **Missing Subsystem** – No adequate mechanism
*   **Adventure-Specific Content** – Not a Tiwas system

#### 4. Baseline Definition

Per your confirmation: Baseline = Canonical/Locked rules + accepted designer rulings + explicitly accepted non-canonical candidate policies as recorded in `Tiwas-Alpha-Playtest-Corpus-2026-09-01.md`. All other material treated as not yet available.

#### 5. Exhaustive Mechanical Inventory – Beyond the Vale

The adventure actually uses/assumes:

*   Core: Attribute rolls ST, DX, Will (with Fearlessness), IQ/Per; Skill rolls Climbing (DX-5 default, -1 per encumbrance), Lockpicking (+2 old stone lock, DX-3 default), Tracking +3 (IQ-2 default), Merchant (IQ-5), Survival (Mountains), First Aid/Physician (IQ-4), generic DX rolls to avoid slip/fall, ST roll to dig, DX roll for glass dagger.
*   Fright Check at -2 (Will-based mental stress)
*   Damage: 1d-4 cr, 1d-3 cut, 1d-1 cr, 1d-2 cr, 2d-2 cr, 1d+2 cut (claws/maw), sw+2 cr, 2d cut; types cr/cut/imp
*   Resources: HP 10-15, FP 11-14, FP loss from cold (1 FP), exertion (2 FP), digging (1 FP), hypothermia damage 1d-3, collapse at FP 0
*   Active Defenses Dodge/Parry/Block, Basic Speed 5.5-6.0, Basic Move 5, Move 5-6
*   Hit location: Torso 0, Arm/Leg -2, Groin -3, Hand -4, Face -5, Skull -7, Vitals -3, Eyes -9; Zero-Step-style swapping not in GURPS but location matters
*   Tactical: Hex map, Speed/Range modifiers Close 0, Short -3, Medium -7, Long -11, Extreme -15, Reach C,1, RoF, Shots, ST, Bulk, Rcl, LC
*   Encumbrance: None=BL, Light=2xBL, Medium=3xBL, Heavy=6xBL, X-Heavy=10xBL; affects Move, Dodge, Climbing
*   Equipment: Weapon stats Damage, Reach, Parry, Skill 13/15/12, ST 12/10, Cost $50-$900, Weight 0.5-6 lbs; Shield DB; Armor DR 2,4 vs crushing, DR 5*; Rope, pitons, torch, tinderbox
*   Hazards: Crumbling statues, icy stairs, falling, gargoyle breaking (grapple failure 1d-1 cr), frozen door
*   Lighting: darkness, inability to light torch → write DARKNESS
*   Traits: Regeneration Fast 1 HP/min only freezing, Regrowth only freezing, DR 2 only freezing, Bad Temper, Appearance Hideous, Claws Sharp, Acute Hearing+2, Luck (reroll twice/hour), Master Builder, Artificer, Fit +1 HT rolls, Magery 3
*   Magic: 20 spells (Apportation, Create Fire, Dark Vision, Extinguish Fire, Fireball 1d-3d, Fireproof, Flame Jet 1d-3d, Flaming Weapon, Heat, Ignite Fire, Keen Vision, Lend Energy, Lend Vitality, Light, Minor Healing, Night Vision, Recover Energy, Sense Danger/Emotion/Foes, Shape Fire, Warmth) with Time 1-2, Duration 1 min to Permanent, Cost 1-4+, energy reduction -1 at skill 15+
*   State flags: Write words AMBUSHED (troll gets first move, starts on blood snow), DARKNESS (penalty), RESCUER (Irak Kul reward $500 + rank)
*   Search: Hidden trap door (shield), antique mace with frozen hand, glass dagger, silver ring $150, etc.
*   Social: Reaction Modifiers Appearance, Status +1, Reputation +1 Artificer etc.
*   Economy: Treasure list $10-$400, sell in Winterhaven
*   Meta-progression: +2 characters base, +1 if defeated 1+ monsters, +1 if loot $500+
*   Creature skills: Brawling-13, Camouflage-12, Stealth-10, Tracking-11

#### 6. Compatibility Analysis

##### 6.1 Playtestable Now (Corpus Part A/B)

These require no new design:

*   All attribute/skill rolls → Tiwas Core Test Transaction (DEC-001, DEC-006). Climbing, Lockpicking, Tracking, Merchant, Survival, First Aid all resolve as ordinary skill tests.
*   Difficulty modifiers → S-8 (DEC-063-066) additive effective Skill modifiers symmetric. Covers Fright Check -2, Tracking +3, Lockpicking +2.
*   Cost = natural roll, Overflow → HP, Recovery floor(Regen/2) → Covers exertion, failure XP (DEC-007, 007.A, 008, 009)
*   Opposed contest and mutual-failure adjudication → Troll Will roll to retreat, contested grapple break (DEC-013, DEC-043)
*   Zero-Step Location Index provider (DEC-014 Locked) and non-attack provenance (DEC-037), final roll governs Extended Tests (DEC-039) → Covers falling location, slip on ice, trap injury location
*   Effect menu structure base tier Inflict Injury HP-only (DEC-023) → All dice damage maps to HP-only
*   Auto-apply + fail-and-fallback to Base (DEC-027, DEC-030)
*   Active Defense voluntary Core Test post-hoc mitigation (DEC-044-050) → Replaces Dodge/Parry/Block
*   Incapacitation HP=0 forced, independent of Wounds, revival via skill tests unlimited (DEC-052-057)
*   Extended Tests Margin-accumulation neutral failure (DEC-067-070) → Breaking ice wall (10 min, 2 FP), digging snow
*   Healing as Extended Test with wound penalty (DEC-071-074) → First Aid recovery loop
*   Movement Speed floor((bsp+bss)/15) (DEC-004) → Abstract movement, not hex-specific

##### 6.2 Playtestable with Substitution

*   FP loss (1 FP cold, 2 FP breaking) → Substitute Physical Energy cost + Overflow to HP (DEC-007). Requires GM to set cost = fixed FP value rather than roll, but mechanically equivalent.
*   Hypothermia 1d-3 damage → Substitute direct HP Condition via systemic exempt (DEC-037) – Tiwas allows direct HP without Location Index for Extreme Temperature, but numeric rate not specified, so GM sets.
*   First Aid restoring 1d-3 HP → Substitute as successful healing test where Margin determines progress; 1d-3 is a GURPS die substitute, not RAW Tiwas but achieves same function.

##### 6.3 Design-Stage Dependencies (Architecture Locked, Content Not)

*   **S-5 Armor Tags/Traits** – Architecture locked (DEC-058-062) but starter Tag vocabulary (e.g., DR 2 cold-only, Bypass relational pairs) not enumerated. The adventure's DR 2 only in freezing cannot be expressed without Tag definitions. Also Armor check at Tier 0 uses Zero-Step read-only per DEC-062, but bypass inapplicable at Tier 0 – partially handles troll's cold-only DR.
*   **S-4 Wound consequences (OPEN-007)** – Ruled that wounds have individual attribute penalties (-1,-5,-30,-80,-100) and healing cost scales, but not table-ready. So Wounded Condition can be imposed, but its mechanical negatives not defined.
*   **S-3 gated-tier Effect contents** – Menu structure ruled but specific Effects for Position, Condition, Equipment, Defense, Location not fully enumerated. Sunder adding permanent Sundered Tag is ruled but its placement confirmation was pending at handoff time.
*   **S-2/S-4 anatomical mapping Tier 2 subdivision** – Procedure locked (DEC-041, DEC-042 secondary d10/d6 no cost) but creature-specific templates per DEC-041 depend on S-12 content authoring. Troll anatomy not authored.
*   **Environmental hazard rates** – DEC-037 exempts Extreme Temperature from Location Index, but no rates for FP loss per hour, hypothermia damage scaling.

##### 6.4 Missing Subsystems (Reserved per DEC-015, No Ruling)

*   **S-12 Creature/NPC Construction** – DEC-076 dual-mode fork: automated = full 24-attribute, tabletop = abbreviated simplified stat-block defaulting to GM decision. No Ice Troll template. This blocks the central combat RAW. Regeneration 1 HP/min only freezing has no Tiwas equivalent; no HP Regen rule (only Energy/MP Regen).
*   **Equipment/Encumbrance/Basic Lift/ST Requirements/Weight/Cost** – Entirely Reserved. Climbing penalty -1 per encumbrance level has no Tiwas counterpart.
*   **Magic / Magery / Spell Energy Economy** – Reserved. 20 spells, energy cost reduction at skill 15+, Time/Duration/Classes (Regular, Area, Missile, Info). Enfys Loom pregen unusable without new subsystem or explicit exclusion ruling.
*   **Numeric DR Soak** – Tiwas explicitly rejects numeric durability/soak pool (DEC-058). GURPS DR 5*, DR 2, DR 4 vs crushing cannot be ported as DR; must be re-authored as Tags.
*   **Speed/Range, Reach, Bulk, RoF, Rcl** – No Tiwas range table; S-8 difficulty grades could substitute but no mapping.
*   **Advantages/Perks/Disadvantages/Quirks/Reaction Modifiers** – No trait system. Luck reroll, Fit +1 HT, Acute Hearing +2, Bad Temper, etc. no mechanics.
*   **Lighting/Torch** – No darkness penalties, torch burn time.
*   **Fright / Mental Conditions** – Condition tier not enumerated, so Fright Check has no Condition to impose.
*   **Treasure Economy / Merchant valuation** – No pricing system.
*   **All-Out Attack, grapple equipment failure, search procedure** – No maneuver list.

##### 6.5 Adventure-Specific Content (Out of Scope)

Branching paragraph numbers (#1-60), story text, word flags RESCUER/AMBUSHED/DARKNESS as narrative keys, map art, specific treasure names (Maiden tapestry $100), Winterhaven reward fiction. These do not indicate Tiwas gaps.

#### 7. System-by-System Gap Matrix

| GURPS System | Tiwas Equivalent | Status |
|---|---|---|
| d100 roll-under + 100 fumble | DEC-001 | Playtestable Now |
| ST/DX/Will/IQ checks | Core Test DEC-006 | Playtestable Now |
| Skill + default (DX-5 etc) | Effective Skill + S-8 mod | Playtestable Now (guess: default handling) |
| Fright Check -2, Tracking +3 | S-8 Difficulty grades DEC-063 | Playtestable Now |
| HP damage dice cr/cut/imp | Base Effect Inflict Injury DEC-023 | Playtestable Now |
| FP loss / exhaustion | Physical Energy + Overflow DEC-007 | With Substitution |
| Active Defense Dodge/Parry/Block | Active Defense DEC-044-050 | Playtestable Now |
| Hit Location + Zero-Step | Zero-Step DEC-014 + DEC-037 | Playtestable Now |
| Extended task digging/ice breaking | S-9/S-10 DEC-067-070 | Playtestable Now |
| Healing / First Aid | S-11 DEC-071-074 | Playtestable Now / Substitution for die amount |
| Incapacitation / Death | S-7 DEC-052-057 | Playtestable Now |
| Opposed Will retreat | S-1 DEC-013 + third-party DEC-043 | Playtestable Now |
| Armor DR 2/5* numeric | Armor Tags only DEC-058 – content missing | Design-Stage Dependency |
| Armor Bypass, Sunder | DEC-059-060 – Tag vocabulary missing | Design-Stage Dependency |
| Wound penalties | OPEN-007 – not table-ready | Design-Stage Dependency |
| Creature template / Troll stats | S-12 DEC-076-077 – no content | Missing Subsystem |
| Regeneration 1 HP/min cold-only | No HP Regen rule | Missing Subsystem |
| Equipment weight / ST req / cost | Reserved DEC-015 | Missing Subsystem |
| Encumbrance + Climbing penalty | Reserved | Missing Subsystem |
| Speed/Range, Reach, Bulk | Reserved | Missing Subsystem |
| Magic 20 spells / Magery / energy | Reserved – no ruling | Missing Subsystem |
| Advantages/Disads/Luck/Fit | Reserved | Missing Subsystem |
| Lighting / DARKNESS | Reserved | Missing Subsystem |
| Fright Conditions | Condition tier not enumerated | Design-Stage Dependency |
| Treasure economy | Content track | Adventure-Specific |

#### 8. Readiness Metric

Out of ~33 distinct mechanical systems actually invoked:

*   Playtestable Now: 14 (42%)
*   Playtestable with Substitution: 3 (9%)
*   Design-Stage Dependency: 5 (15%)
*   Missing Subsystem: 11 (33%)

If Enfys Loom (spellcaster) is excluded and troll is run via GM-decision abbreviated stat block per DEC-076 tabletop mode, playable coverage rises to ~70% for exploration/combat loop. With full RAW including magic and numeric DR, coverage drops to ~51%.

#### 9. Recommendations – Prioritized to Unblock This Adventure

1.  **P0 – S-12 Creature Authoring:** Author Ice Troll abbreviated tabletop stat block using dual-mode ruling. Decide HP, Physical Energy, Skill Caps, Tag for cold-only DR, Regeneration as Condition-tier Effect. This alone unblocks the climax.
2.  **P0 – Equipment/Encumbrance Minimal Viable Rule:** Lock minimal rules for weight, ST requirement, and encumbrance penalty to climbing/Move. Even a single table (BL = floor(bpp/…)?) would satisfy the module's -1 per level.
3.  **P0 – Magic Scope Decision:** For this playtest, rule Enfys Loom as out-of-scope OR approve a non-canonical fire-only cantrip list using existing Core Test costs. Do not attempt full GURPS Magic conversion.
4.  **P1 – Armor Tag Vocabulary Starter Set:** Define at least three Tags: `Cold-Hardened DR`, `Shield Medium Bear`, `Claws Sharp` and one Bypass pair to test DEC-059.
5.  **P1 – Wound Consequence Minimal Table:** Publish OPEN-007 magnitudes as table-ready for this playtest: e.g., Wounded -1 = -5 effective Skill, -5 = -15, etc., to test healing.
6.  **P2 – Environmental Hazard Rates:** Define Extreme Temperature systemic direct HP rule: 1 Physical Energy loss per 30 min, Overflow as cold damage, Survival (Mountains) mitigates via S-8 difficulty shift.

#### 10. Risks and Limitations

*   Tiwas corpus explicitly warns S-3 gated-tier contents not enumerated – using Sunder or Disarm in this adventure would be GM fiat.
*   Overflow-immutability (DEC-007.A) means Armor Tags can never modify Overflow, conflicting with GURPS expectation that DR reduces all damage including fatigue-derived HP loss. Must be communicated to playtesters.
*   Hex tactical combat not supported; using theater-of-mind loses Speed/Range depth of original.

#### 11. Conclusion

Beyond the Vale of Madness is an ideal stress-test: it is simple enough that Tiwas's Locked Core (d100, 9-step transaction, S-1 opposed contest, Zero-Step) already handles >50% of rolls. The blockers are not the dungeon's branching logic but Tiwas's intentionally Reserved systems: creatures, equipment, and magic. Completing S-12 content authoring for a single creature and a minimal equipment/encumbrance ruling would move this adventure from "requires heavy GM fiat" to "playtestable now" for martial characters, providing a clean development metric for Tiwas completion.

---
**Appendix – Flagged Guesses:** Default skill handling mapped to effective skill; FP→Physical Energy mapping; hex map optional; treasure economy treated as adventure-specific not system gap.


```

## btvmadness-adapt-report-metaai-2.md
> **LLM attribution:** Muse Spark 1.1 - report 2

```markdown
# Tiwas TTRPG – Adventure Readiness Audit
## Using Beyond the Vale of Madness (GURPS) as External Benchmark

**Document Control**
| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Document | Tiwas Adventure Readiness Audit – Beyond the Vale of Madness |
| Version | 1.0 – Full Audit per Tailored Prompt |
| Date | 2026-09-01 |
| Adventure Source | `Beyond-the-Vale-of-Madness-GURPS.pdf` – J.C. Connors v1.2, 19 pages, 60 numbered paragraphs, 2 pregens |
| Tiwas Baseline | `Tiwas-Alpha-Playtest-Corpus-2026-09-01.md` – Part A Canonical/Locked DEC-001–016 + Part B Ruled non-canonical DEC-023–074. Part C = NOT table-ready |
| Authority Model | `governance/status-model.md` – Canonical/Locked authoritative, Non-canonical designer ruling real but not Canonical, Draft/Open not table-ready |
| Auditor Role | Tiwas Systems Readiness Auditor and Adaptation Analyst |

### 1. Executive Summary

Beyond the Vale of Madness was selected because it is intentionally minimal: a solo Choose-Your-Path with one mandatory attrition loop, one major monster (Ice Troll), one optional horror monster (Blood Man), basic dungeon exploration, and two 150-point pregens – one martial dwarf (King Coppertong) and one spellcaster (Enfys Loom, Magery 3, 20 spells).

**Tiwas can already resolve the core gameplay loop:** Standard skill tests for all GURPS attribute/skill rolls, difficulty-modified effective skill, resource cost = natural roll with Overflow→HP, Failure XP and Skill Roll Pool, universal opposed contest S-1 with third-party mutual-failure adjudication, Zero-Step Location Index for attack and non-attack falls, base Effect Inflict Injury HP-only with auto-apply and fallback, Active Defense as voluntary post-hoc mitigation, forced incapacitation at HP 0 with unlimited revival attempts, and Extended Tests via Margin-accumulation for breaking ice / digging snow.

**Tiwas cannot yet run the adventure RAW without GM fiat for:**

* S-12 creature templates – no Ice Troll or Blood Man stat blocks exist; DEC-076 dual-mode tabletop path defaults to GM decision
* Equipment / Encumbrance / Basic Lift / ST requirement / weight / cost – Reserved per DEC-015
* Numeric DR soak armor – Tiwas S-5 architecture is Tags/Traits only, no soak pool per DEC-058; content vocabulary missing
* Magic – Magery, energy cost, spell maintenance, missile/area/regular classes – Reserved, no ruling
* Wound consequence magnitudes, Condition-tier contents, environmental hazard rates – architecture locked but contents not table-ready

**Readiness:** Martial path (King Coppertong, cave or snow route, courtyard search, servant/guardhouse) = ~70% Playtestable Now or With Adaptation Mapping if troll uses abbreviated GM block. Full adventure including Enfys Loom spellcasting and strict RAW troll regeneration = Not Playtestable Now; blocked by Missing Subsystems S-12 and Magic.

### 2. Audit Objective and Method

**Objective:** Use an external published module to produce a development diagnostic metric: which Tiwas subsystems are sufficient for play, which are design-stage, which are missing.

**Method:**
1.  Extract every mechanical demand from all 19 pages: all paragraphs #1-60, treasure list, character sheets pages 14-19, battle maps, monster blocks.
2.  Classify demand mechanically, not procedurally: "What does this require the system to accomplish?" per tailored prompt. All GURPS skill/attribute rolls = ordinary Tiwas Core Test per constraint.
3.  Map against Tiwas corpus authority: Only Part A (DEC-001-016 Locked) and Part B (DEC-023-074 Ruled) counted as available. Part C (Open, wound consequences, S-3 gated-tier contents) = unavailable.
4.  Preserve Core invariants DEC-016 and 9-step transaction DEC-006.

### 3. Source Adventure Mechanical Inventory

#### 3.1 Core Resolution
* Attribute rolls: ST to dig (#10), DX to light torch (#7), DX to pry glass dagger (#24), DX to navigate ice stairs (#25,#29,#50), DX to jump pit (#55), Will with Fearlessness modifier (#2), Will for searching bloody room (#48)
* Skill rolls with defaults: Climbing (DX-5, -1 per encumbrance, -3 wall) (#15,#35), Lockpicking +2 old stone lock (DX-3) (#18), Tracking +3 (IQ-2) (#27), Merchant IQ-5 (#23), Survival (Mountains) (#19), First Aid/Physician IQ-4 (#21,#37), Search/Perception-5 (#30,#43), Forced Entry ST-4 (#40), Stealth vs Per 12 Quick Contest (#44), Tactics IQ-6 for tactical start (#38)
* Fright Check at -2 (#58, Blood Man #38)
* Failure outcomes: damage, state flags, branch loss

#### 3.2 Damage, Resources, Hazards
* Damage dice: 1d-4 cut (#16), 1d-3 cut/hypothermia (#19,#24), 1d-1 cr fall (#5), 1d-2 cr stairs (#25,#29), 2d-2 cr cliff (#15,#52), 1d+2 cut claws/maw (#17), sw+2 cr mace (#2), 1d cor blood seep (#46)
* Types: cr, cut, imp, cor (corrosive)
* HP 10-15, FP 11-14, FP costs: -1 FP digging, -1 FP cold, -2 FP breaking ice, collapse at FP 0 (#10,#19,#21)
* Environmental: cold winds, hypothermia 1d-3, icy stairs, crumbling statues, gargoyle break
* Falling: cliff, wall, pit – soft armor only protects (#5)

#### 3.3 Combat Systems
* Monster blocks: Ice Troll ST15 HP15 Speed6.5 DX12 Will11 Move6 IQ7 Per12 SM0 HT12 FP12 DR2 Dodge9 Claws13 1d+2 cut Reach C,1 Maw13 1d+2 cut; Traits Appearance Hideous, Bad Temper, Claws Sharp, Regeneration Fast 1HP/min only freezing, Regrowth only freezing, DR2 only freezing; Skills Brawling-13, Camouflage-12, Stealth-10, Tracking-11. Blood Man ST13 HP15 Speed6.0 DX12 Will11 Move6 IQ9 Per11 SM0 HT12 FP12 DR1 Dodge10 Parry10 Claws14 1d cut, Blood Seep 1d-2 cor/sec Large-Area Injury average torso + least protected; Traits Appearance Monstrous, Combat Reflexes, Injury Tolerance Homogenous, Night Vision 6; Skills Brawling-14, Camouflage-12, Stealth-12.
* Maneuvers: All-Out Attack double claw no defense when troll below 0 HP and passes Will, trading blows vs hex tactical map (optional per #17,#38)
* Active defenses, Basic Lift/Encumbrance, Speed/Range table (-3 close etc), Hit locations -2 to -9 (Vitals -3, Eyes -9)
* Will roll for troll retreat at 0 HP (#17)

#### 3.4 Equipment, Lighting, Search
* Weapons: ST12, Axe/Mace skill, weight 5 lbs (#2), crossbow + 1 bolt (#59), fine axe, throwing axe, knife, broadsword
* Armor: mail DR4/2 vs crushing, leather DR2, shoes DR1, shield DB medium with 3 white bears (#13)
* Consumables: torch, tinderbox, rope 10 yards, pitons, blanket, waterskin
* Lighting: DARKNESS flag → -3 attack vs Blood Man unless Night Vision (#7,#38)
* Search: courtyard search, trap door shield (#13), signet ring, antique mace with frozen hand, glass dagger Shatterproof enchanted, tiara, tapestries $10/$100, scepter $400

#### 3.5 Magic
* Enfys Loom: Magery3, 20 spells: Apportation, Create Fire, Dark Vision, Extinguish Fire, Fireball 1d-9d missile, Fireproof area, Flame Jet 1d-3d, Flaming Weapon, Heat, Ignite Fire, Keen Vision, Lend Energy/Vitality, Light, Minor Healing, Night Vision, Recover Energy Special, Sense Danger/Emotion/Foes, Shape Fire, Warmth. Time1-2, Duration 1 sec to Permanent, Cost1-4+, maintenance half cost, energy reduction -1 at skill15+. Powerstone bracelet 3pts, spell gestures/words rules.

#### 3.6 Social, Economy, Progression
* Advantages/Perks: Acute Hearing+2, Acute Touch+2, Luck reroll twice/hour, Master Builder1, Artificer1, Status+1, Wealth Comfortable, Dwarf package (DR1, Lifting ST+2, Extended Lifespan, Longevity, Resistant Poison+3, Greed, Intolerance Orcs, Miserliness, -1 Move, Stubbornness, Signature Gear), Fit +1 HT rolls, Charisma1, Magery3
* Disadvantages/Quirks: Delusion, Honesty, Klutz, One Hand -4, Enemy, Impulsiveness, Obsession, Pacifism Cannot harm innocents, Loves reading etc.
* Reaction modifiers: Status, Reputation +1 Artificer, Appearance, Honesty
* Languages: Dwarvish Native, Anglish Accented/Literate
* Economy: Treasure values $25-$400, bazaar selling, RESCUER reward $500 Martyrs of War rank
* Progression: +2 chars base, +1 if defeated monster, +1 if >$500 loot (#60)

#### 3.7 State Tracking
* Words: AMBUSHED (troll gets first move, start on blood snow hex) (#20), DARKNESS (#7), RESCUER (#32)

### 4. System-by-System Tiwas Compatibility Assessment

| # | Adventure Requirement | Where Used | Tiwas Capability | Authority / Status | Readiness | Blocking Significance | Remaining Work |

| 26 | Fright / mental stress | Fright Check -2 #58 | Condition tier not enumerated beyond Wounded; S-3 gated-tier contents missing | Not table-ready | Design-Stage Dependency | Low | Define Frightened Condition |
| 27 | Search / hidden objects | Trap door #13, courtyard #43 | Search as ordinary Core Test | Playtestable Now | None | None | None |
| 28 | State flags AMBUSHED/RESCUER | Tactical start, $500 reward | No explicit flag system but can be Condition/Tag + narrative | Adaptation Mapping Required | Low | None | Use Tags/Conditions |
| 29 | Economy / progression | Treasure $10-$400, $500 loot bonus | Content authoring, not system; General XP DEC-011 covers adventure awards | Content | Adventure Content Only | None | None |

### 5. Playtestability Matrix

| Category | Count | % | Examples |
|---|---|---|---|
| Playtestable Now | 15 | 52% | d100, Core Test, S-8 modifiers, S-1 opposed, Zero-Step attack+non-attack, Inflict Injury, Active Defense, Incapacitation, Extended Tests, Healing structure, Search |
| Adaptation Mapping Required | 3 | 10% | Default skills, state flags, economy – need adventure-specific mapping using existing Tiwas primitives |
| Design-Stage Dependency | 6 | 21% | Armor Tags vocabulary, Sunder, Wound consequences OPEN-007, S-3 gated-tier Condition/Position contents, Lighting/Darkness, Fright |
| Missing Subsystem | 5 | 17% | S-12 creature templates, Regeneration, Equipment/Encumbrance, Speed/Range tactical, Magic/Magery |
| Adventure Content Only | — | — | Paragraph structure, named treasures, NPC names Jrak Kul, map art |

### 6. Missing / Design-Stage Dependencies (Prioritized)

**Critical Blockers:**

* **S-12 Creature Authoring** – DEC-076 allows tabletop abbreviated stat-block defaulting to GM decision, but without an authored block, Ice Troll cannot be run RAW. Remaining work: Author single creature template using individual-template anatomy per DEC-041; define Body attributes sum to HP 15 equivalent; assign Skills Brawling-13 etc as Tier-2 Advanced Skills; assign Tags: `Claws Sharp`, `Maw Shark`, `Hideous`, `Cold-Bound Regeneration`, `Cold-Bound DR2`.
* **Magic System Exclusion** – Enfys Loom requires Magery3 + 20 spells. Per DEC-015 Reserved, no magic system exists. Remaining work: Design decision – exclude Enfys from this benchmark (use King Coppertong only) OR approve non-canonical minimal fire magic ruling that reuses Core Test costs (e.g., Fireball = Inflict Injury with MP cost = roll).

**High Blockers:**

* **Equipment/Encumbrance** – Required for Climbing -1 per level (#15), tapestry ST14 carry limit (#51), weapon ST requirements. Remaining work: Minimal encumbrance tiers mapping to Physical Energy or Movement Speed penalty.
* **Armor Tag Vocabulary** – S-5 architecture locked but no Tag list. Remaining work: Starter vocabulary + one Bypass pair to test cold-only DR.
* **Wound Consequence Table (OPEN-007)** – Structure ruled (individual penalties -1,-5,-30 etc) but magnitudes not table-ready. Remaining work: Publish minimal table for this playtest.

**Medium Blockers:**

* **Lighting/Darkness and Fright** – Condition tier contents not enumerated. Remaining work: Define two Conditions: `Darkness-Blinded (-3 attack)` and `Frightened` as Condition-tier Effects gated by Quality.

### 7. Adventure Path Dependency Analysis

All paths share Core Tests and Extended Tests, so base exploration is Playtestable Now.

* **Path A – Cave Route (#12→#9→#31→#8/#17):** Requires Stealth Quick Contest vs Per12 (#44) → Playtestable Now via S-1. Requires troll combat → blocked by S-12. Requires digging ST roll + FP loss #10 → Playtestable Now with Adaptation (ST = Might skill).
* **Path B – Snow Trek (#1→#19→#3):** Requires Survival (Mountains) and hypothermia 1d-3 + FP loss → Playtestable Now with Adaptation (systemic exempt direct HP per DEC-037).
* **Path C – Wall Climb (#35→#42→#4/#45):** Requires Climbing -3, rope/grapple, gargoyle break 1d-1 cr → Equipment/Encumbrance missing, but climb itself Playtestable Now.
* **Path D – Guardhouse (#11→#18→#30→#13→#6):** Lockpicking +2, Forced Entry ST-4, Search → Playtestable Now. Trap door shield → Adaptation Mapping (shield as Tag).
* **Path E – Servant Entrance (#14→#16→#24→#33):** Glass dagger DX roll + 1d-4 cut → Playtestable Now. Tapestries Merchant roll #23, ST14 carry #51 → Encumbrance missing.
* **Path F – Blood Man (#7→#38→#48→#56):** Fright Check, DARKNESS -3, Tactics, Night Vision, Large-Area Injury, Injury Tolerance Homogenous, Night Vision 6 → Design-Stage Dependencies (Fright Condition, Darkness, Homogenous as Tag) + Missing Regeneration-like tolerance.
* **Path G – RESCUER (#37→#32):** First Aid → Playtestable Now.

**Shortest successful path** (#1→#19→#3→#14→#33→#23→#55→#7→#38→#48→#60) still requires: Survival, DX rolls, Fright Check, Blood Man combat (S-12 + Conditions), Will roll. Therefore even shortest path blocked by S-12 and Condition contents.

### 8. Tiwas Readiness Assessment

Tiwas Alpha-Playtest Corpus 2026-09-01 provides a complete core resolution loop and advanced subsystems S-1 through S-11 architecturally, but leaves content authoring for creatures, equipment, armor Tags, and wound magnitudes as Open.

Using this adventure as benchmark:

* **Core exploration loop (skill tests, difficulty, overflow, extended tasks, search):** Ready.
* **Combat loop vs intelligent monster with conditional regeneration and retreat Will roll:** Not ready without S-12 content + Tag vocabulary.
* **Horror sub-loop (Blood Man with darkness penalty, corrosive seep, homogenous tolerance):** Not ready – requires Condition contents + armor/homogenous Tags.
* **Spellcaster loop:** Not ready – Magic reserved.
* **Overall metric:** 52% Playtestable Now, 10% Adaptation Mapping Required, 38% blocked by Design-Stage or Missing. This adventure exposes that S-12 content authoring and Equipment/Encumbrance are the next critical path for any dungeon-style playtest.

### 9. Development Priorities Exposed by the Adventure

1.  **P0 – Author S-12 Minimal Creature:** Ice Troll abbreviated block using DEC-076 tabletop mode. Include anatomy template per DEC-041 (Head/Torso/Arms/Legs weighted ranges) and left/right parity odd/even. This unblocks #17.
2.  **P0 – Magic Scope Decision Document:** Explicitly exclude Enfys Loom from readiness metric or approve fire-only minimal ruling (Fireball = Missile Effect Inflict Injury, cost = MP = natural roll, maintains 9-step transaction).
3.  **P1 – Minimal Equipment/Encumbrance Ruling:** Define Basic Lift from bpp (Bear Might) and encumbrance tiers affecting Climbing effective skill and Movement Speed. Covers #15, #35, #51.
4.  **P1 – Armor Tag Starter Vocabulary:** Define Tags: `DR2 Cold-Bound`, `DR1`, `Leather`, `Homogenous (halves impaling?)`, `Sharp Claws`, `Corrosive Seep`. Define one Bypass pair `Sharp vs Mail` to test DEC-059.
5.  **P1 – Publish OPEN-007 as Table-Ready Minimal:** e.g., Wound -1 = -5 effective skill on location, -5 = -15 and requires Extended Test healing target 20 Margin, -30 = incapacitation risk, etc. Allows testing of Wounded Condition via Impose Condition Effect #2.
6.  **P2 – Condition Tier Minimal Contents:** Define `Darkness-Blinded`, `Frightened`, `Sundered` (per DEC-060 permanent Tag) as Condition-tier Effects gated by Quality per DEC-031.
7.  **P2 – Environmental Hazard Rates:** Define Extreme Temperature systemic: -1 Physical Energy per 30 min exposure, failed Survival increases effective difficulty one grade, Overflow as hypothermia HP.

### 10. Conclusions

Beyond the Vale of Madness successfully functions as a readiness benchmark because it is not combat-heavy but still touches almost every Reserved system in Tiwas. Tiwas's Locked Core and Ruled subsystems S-1, S-2, S-3 menu structure, S-4 trigger logic, S-5/S-6 architecture, S-7, S-8, S-9/S-10, S-11 are sufficient for skill-based exploration and abstract combat.

The adventure proves that **system architecture is not the blocker; content authoring is.** S-12 creature templates, equipment/encumbrance values, armor Tag vocabulary, and wound magnitudes are all recognized as needed but have no table-ready instances. Magic is a deliberate exclusion requiring a scoping decision.

Completing P0 and P1 above would move this adventure from 52% to ~85% Playtestable Now, providing a clean go/no-go metric for Tiwas alpha playtesting.

### 11. Source and Evidence Register

* Adventure mechanical demands: PDF pages 1-10 core paragraphs, page 4 Ice Troll block, page 8 Blood Man block, pages 12-13 treasure and thanks, pages 14-19 character sheets King Coppertong (150pt dwarf, Axe/Mace13, Merchant13, ST12 requirement) and Enfys Loom (150pt, Magery3, 20 spells list with Time/Duration/Cost), battle maps pages 14-15.
* Tiwas authority: Corpus §A.1-A.16 DEC-001-016 Canonical Locked; §B.1 DEC-013+DEC-031 Quality gating; §B.2 DEC-014 Zero-Step + DEC-037 non-attack provenance + DEC-039 final roll governs; §B.3 DEC-023-030 Effect menu, DEC-028 combined Tag+Location gating narrowed by DEC-041; §B.4 DEC-032-036,041-042 Wound/Injury, universal Skill-Tier≥2 gate, secondary roll no cost; §B.5 DEC-058-062 Armor Tags only, Bypass relational, location-bound; §B.6 DEC-044-050 Active Defense voluntary post-hoc; §B.7 DEC-052-057 HP0 forced incapacitation; §B.8 DEC-043 third-party adjudication inverted Failure Margin, DEC-063-066 difficulty grades additive skill-side, DEC-051 Stakes Gate rejected; §B.9 DEC-067-070 Extended Tests margin-accumulation neutral; §B.10 DEC-071-074 S-11 healing explicit test + wound penalty; Part C OPEN-007 not table-ready, S-3 gated-tier contents not enumerated, S-12 DEC-076-077 framework only.

### 12. Explicit Assumptions / Unresolved Questions

1.  Assumed any GURPS skill/attribute roll = ordinary Tiwas Core Test per your instruction; did not attempt to preserve GURPS critical success/failure tables.
2.  Assumed GURPS default penalties (DX-5, IQ-4) map to S-8 effective skill modifiers rather than separate untrained rule; Tiwas has no explicit default rule.
3.  Assumed hex tactical map optional because adventure text states "You can trade blows with the troll, or jump into tactical combat using the battle map" – treated as Adaptation Mapping Required, not Missing.
4.  Assumed FP costs (1 FP, 2 FP) can be substituted via Physical Energy cost + Overflow, though Tiwas Cost = natural roll per DEC-007, not fixed value.
5.  Assumed treasure economy and character point progression (+2 chars etc) is Adventure Content Only, not Tiwas system gap; General XP DEC-011 covers awards.
6.  Unresolved: Does Luck reroll mechanic violate Invariant 17 (no competing resource) or can it be modeled as Tag? No ruling.
7.  Unresolved: Should cold-only DR2 and Regeneration be modeled as Conditions, Tags with situational activation, or as part of creature template anatomy? Requires Tiwa decision.
8.  Unresolved: Should Enfys Loom be excluded from this benchmark metric or should minimal fire magic be approved as non-canonical ruling for testing?
```

## btvmadness-adapt-report-mimo-2-5-1.md
> **LLM attribution:** Mimo 2.5 - report 1

```markdown
# Prompt Draft

**Role:** You are a systems-readiness analyst producing a formal project report for the Tiwas TTRPG development team.

**Context:** We are assessing Tiwas TTRPG's readiness for playtesting by mechanically analysing a published GURPS solo adventure ("Beyond the Vale of Madness" by J.C. Connors). The goal is to identify every game-mechanical system the adventure uses or assumes, then map each against Tiwas's current canonical rules and accepted designer rulings to determine what is playtestable now versus what still needs development. This creates a repeatable readiness metric for Tiwas's development pipeline.

**Audience:** The Tiwas designer (Tiwa) and development team, using the report to prioritise remaining subsystem work.

**Format:** Formal project report in markdown. Structured sections with tables. Approximately 2000–3000 words.

**Success criteria:**
1. Every mechanical system the adventure uses is identified and categorised — nothing silently omitted.
2. Each system is mapped to Tiwas's current state with specific DEC/rule citations where applicable.
3. The report produces an actionable dependency map showing exactly which Tiwas subsystems must be completed before full playtesting is possible.

**Constraints:**
- Assess mechanically (what does the adventure require the rules to accomplish?) not procedurally (how to convert GURPS rules).
- Treat the Tiwas corpus (canonical rules + accepted designer rulings) as the authoritative baseline.
- Do not speculate beyond what the provided content states.

**Examples:** The two provided documents (adventure module and Tiwas corpus) are the source material.

---

# Beyond the Vale of Madness → Tiwas TTRPG: Systems-Readiness Assessment Report

**Document Type:** Formal Project Report
**Date:** 2026-09-01
**Analyst:** MiMo (LLM)
**Source Materials:** "Beyond the Vale of Madness" (J.C. Connors, GURPS solo adventure, v1.2); "Tiwas — Alpha-Playtest Corpus" (v1.0, 2026-09-01)
**Purpose:** Identify every game-mechanical system the adventure uses or assumes, map each against Tiwas's current ruleset, and determine which systems are playtestable now versus which require further development.

---

## 1. Executive Summary

"Beyond the Vale of Madness" is a solo "Choose Your Path" adventure designed for GURPS Fantasy / Dungeon Fantasy. It exercises a moderate range of mechanical systems: core resolution, attribute/skill checks, damage, HP tracking, fatigue, combat (melee and ranged), monster stat blocks, equipment, environmental hazards, fear mechanics, healing, armour, weapon proficiency, encumbrance, and tactical map-based combat.

Of the **18 distinct mechanical systems** identified in the adventure, **4 are playtestable now** under current Tiwas rules, **3 are playtestable with adaptation** (existing Tiwas mechanisms can substitute), **6 are design-stage dependencies** (Tihas has identified/proposed the subsystem but it is not sufficiently resolved), and **5 are missing subsystems** (Tihas has no adequate mechanism). The core resolution loop and basic skill-check gameplay are functional; the primary gaps are in combat subsystems (active defence, armour, wounds, incapacitation), equipment/encumbrance, and creature/NPC construction.

---

## 2. Methodology

1. **Extraction.** Every game-mechanical system referenced, invoked, or assumed by the adventure text was catalogued — including systems mentioned only in passing (e.g., encumbrance penalties on climbing).
2. **Mapping.** Each extracted system was matched against the Tiwas corpus: Part A (Core Locked Rules, DEC-001–DEC-016) and Part B (Ruled non-canonical subsystems, DEC-023–DEC-077).
3. **Classification.** Each system was assigned a readiness category (see §3).
4. **Dependency analysis.** Systems were cross-referenced to identify blocking dependencies — i.e., which Tiwas subsystems must be completed before the adventure is fully playable.

---

## 3. Readiness Categories

| Category | Definition |
|---|---|
| **Playtestable Now** | Tiwas has sufficient locked or ruled rules to run this system without substitution or gap-filling. |
| **Playtestable with Adaptation** | The GURPS mechanism can be functionally replaced by an existing Tiwas mechanism; no new subsystem design required, but a mapping/conversion decision is needed. |
| **Design-Stage Dependency** | Tiwas has identified and/or proposed the necessary subsystem, but it is not sufficiently resolved to rely on in play (e.g., partially ruled, contents not enumerated, or gated on unresolved dependencies). |
| **Missing Subsystem** | Tiwas has no adequate mechanism; a new subsystem must be designed. |
| **Adventure-Specific Content** | Not a Tiwas system (setting, narrative, flavour text); outside system-readiness scope. |

---

## 4. Systems Inventory and Readiness Assessment

### 4.1 Core Resolution Mechanic

**Adventure usage:** All skill and attribute checks use GURPS's roll-under d6 system (3d6 ≤ Skill/Attribute). The adventure invokes this dozens of times across every paragraph.

**Tiwas equivalent:** DEC-001 (d100 roll-under), DEC-006 (9-step Core Test Transaction). Fully locked, canonical.

**Readiness: Playtestable Now.** The core resolution loop is complete and locked. Any GURPS skill check maps to a Tiwas Core Test: roll d100, compare to Skill, pay Cost = natural roll, resolve Failure XP, recover. The die size differs (d100 vs. 3d6) but the functional requirement (roll-under resolution with success/failure determination) is fully met.

---

### 4.2 Attribute System

**Adventure usage:** Checks against DX (dexterity), ST (strength), IQ (intelligence), Will, HT (health). Used for skill checks, damage calculations, and resistance.

**Tiwas equivalent:** DEC-003 (24-Attribute Matrix). Twelve Body attributes (Power/Speed/Endurance × Power/Speed/Endurance/Social) and twelve Mind attributes, each generated as 1d100. Fully locked, canonical.

**Readiness: Playtestable with Adaptation.** Tiwas's 24-attribute system is more granular than GURPS's 6 attributes. A mapping decision is required: e.g., GURPS DX → Tiwas Body/Speed attributes (bsp, bss, bse); GURPS ST → Body/Power (bpp, bps, bpe); GURPS IQ → Mind attributes. This is a conversion task, not a design gap.

---

### 4.3 Skill System

**Adventure usage:** Invokes numerous skills — Climbing, Survival (Mountains), Lockpicking, Search, Perception (Vision), First Aid, Physician, Merchant, Tracking, Fearlessness, WAIL, weapon skills (Axe/Mace), and implicit untrained checks (DX-5, IQ-4, IQ-5, etc.).

**Tiwas equivalent:** DEC-005 (Skills: Tier/Cap/Starting Value). Skills are Tier-1 through Tier-24, Cap = floor(sum of attributes / Tier), Starting Value = floor(Cap / 2). Fully locked, canonical.

**Readiness: Playtestable Now.** Tiwas's skill system is complete and locked. Each GURPS skill maps to a Tiwas skill with a defined attribute formula. Untrained checks (GURPS DX-5, IQ-4) map to Tiwas's default of Skill 0 or a GM-assigned base. The skill list itself is adventure-specific content (which skills exist and what they cover) rather than a system gap.

---

### 4.4 Damage System

**Adventure usage:** Multiple damage expressions — `1d-1`, `1d-2`, `1d-3`, `1d-4`, `2d-2`, `sw-2 cr` (swing minus 2, crushing), `1d+2 cut` (claws), `1d cut` (blood man claws). Damage types referenced: crushing (cr), cutting (cut).

**Tiwas equivalent:** DEC-007 (Resource Cost and Overflow). Overflow = Cost − Remaining Resource, applied as direct HP damage. DEC-032 (Injury = HP Damage). DEC-033 (Wounds as distinct from HP). DEC-023 (S-3 Effect menu: Inflict Injury is base-tier HP-only Effect).

**Readiness: Design-Stage Dependency.** Tiwas's damage model is structurally different from GURPS. GURPS uses weapon-die-plus-modifier damage applied against DR; Tiwas uses Overflow (roll-cost exceeding resource pool) as the HP-damage mechanism, plus S-3 Effect-based Injury. The *functional requirement* (reducing HP) is met, but the *how* is fundamentally different. Specific gaps:
- No weapon damage dice or weapon stats exist (equipment subsystem is Reserved, DEC-015).
- No damage type differentiation (crushing vs. cutting) exists.
- The Overflow mechanism handles HP reduction but doesn't replicate GURPS's weapon-vs-armour damage pipeline.

---

### 4.5 Hit Points and Resource Pools

**Adventure usage:** HP tracking throughout (damage, healing, death at 0 HP). Fatigue Points (FP) — reduced by exertion, cold exposure, digging; collapse at FP 0.

**Tiwas equivalent:** DEC-004 (HP = sum of 12 Body attributes; MP = sum of 12 Mind attributes; Physical Energy = bep + bes + bee). DEC-007 (Overflow → HP damage). DEC-008 (Recovery after every test).

**Readiness: Playtestable with Adaptation.** HP is fully defined. FP has a direct analogue in Physical Energy (bep + bes + bee). The adventure's FP reductions for exertion/cold map to Physical Energy expenditure or direct HP loss. MP exists as a parallel resource for Mind skills. The functional requirement (tracking a depletable physical resource pool) is met.

---

### 4.6 Combat — Attack and Damage Resolution

**Adventure usage:** Paragraph #17 presents full combat with the Ice Troll. Includes attack rolls, damage rolls, monster attack stats (Ice Claws: 1d+2 cut, Reach C,1; Shark-toothed Maw: 1d+2 cut, Reach C), HP thresholds (troll retreats at 0 HP if Will roll fails), All-Out Attack option (two claw attacks, no defense).

**Tiwas equivalent:** DEC-006 (Core Test Transaction), DEC-013 (S-1 Opposed Contest), DEC-023 (S-3 Effect menu). The attack-vs-defence loop would use S-1 Opposed Contest with S-3 Effect selection on success.

**Readiness: Design-Stage Dependency.** The opposed-contest primitive (S-1) is locked and the Effect menu structure (S-3) is ruled, but:
- **Active Defence (S-6)** is ruled but not yet playtested; the defender makes a genuine Core Test (DEC-044), but the full interaction with S-3 Effects, S-5 Armour, and S-4 Wounds has never been exercised together.
- **Effect contents** within gated tiers (Position, Condition, Equipment, Defence, Location) are **not fully enumerated** (Part C, §C.3). The base tier (Inflict Injury HP-only, Open Retreat/Compel Yield) is available, but the adventure requires more granular combat outcomes.
- **Weapon stats and attack skills** do not exist (equipment subsystem Reserved).
- **Reach, range bands, tactical positioning** have no Tiwas equivalent.

---

### 4.7 Monster/NPC Stat Blocks

**Adventure usage:** Two full stat blocks provided — Ice Troll (ST 15, DX 12, IQ 7, HT 12, HP 15, various skills and traits) and Blood Man (ST 13, DX 12, IQ 9, HT 12, HP 15, special ability: Blood Step). Both include derived stats (Speed, Move, Dodge, Parry, DR, SM).

**Tiwas equivalent:** DEC-076 (S-12 Ruling A — dual-mode fork: automated systems use full 24-attribute generation; tabletop uses abbreviated stat blocks with GM-decided resolution). DEC-077 (S-12 Ruling B — content authoring via playtesting, not advisory drafting).

**Readiness: Missing Subsystem.** No creature stat blocks, templates, or NPC construction rules exist. DEC-076/077 establish the *path* (abbreviated stat blocks for tabletop; content developed via playtesting) but provide zero mechanical content. The adventure requires at least two fully statted opponents.

---

### 4.8 Armour and Damage Resistance

**Adventure usage:** Ice Troll has DR 2 (only in freezing temperatures). Blood Man has DR 1. Shield found in guardhouse (steel-plated medium shield). Implicit armour use by the player character.

**Tiwas equivalent:** DEC-058–DEC-062 (S-5 Armour). Architecture is Tags/Traits only (no numeric soak). Bypass is relational Tag-pair matching. Sunder adds a permanent Tag. Coverage is location-bound. Resolution sequence: Effect → Armour Tags → Active Defence.

**Readiness: Design-Stage Dependency.** S-5 is ruled but:
- No Armour Tags have been defined (the Tag vocabulary is an open dependency).
- The interaction with S-6 Active Defence has never been playtested.
- GURPS's numeric DR model has no direct Tiwas equivalent; the functional requirement (reducing incoming harm) must be achieved through the Tags/Traits system, which has no content yet.

---

### 4.9 Weapon Proficiency and Equipment

**Adventure usage:** Antique mace (sw-2 cr, requires Axe/Mace skill, ST 12, 5 lbs). Glass dagger (enchanted Shatterproof). Iron shortbow (discarded as broken). Steel-plated medium shield. Signet ring. Troll teeth. Rope and grapple. Torch/tundrabox. Dagger used for lockpicking.

**Tiwas equivalent:** DEC-015 (Reserved Systems — equipment explicitly outside the locked Core).

**Readiness: Missing Subsystem.** No equipment rules exist. No weapon stats, no item Tags, no weight/encumbrance values, no enchantment mechanics. The adventure requires functional weapons and at least basic inventory tracking.

---

### 4.10 Encumbrance

**Adventure usage:** Paragraph #15 explicitly states "Climbing rolls are penalized at -1 per encumbrance level." Implicit throughout (carrying items, weight of weapons).

**Tiwas equivalent:** DEC-015 (Reserved — encumbrance explicitly listed as outside the locked Core).

**Readiness: Missing Subsystem.** No encumbrance rules exist.

---

### 4.11 Environmental Hazards — Cold/Exposure

**Adventure usage:** Repeated FP loss from cold exposure (paragraphs #1, #19, #21). Hypothermia damage (1d-3 from failed Survival roll, #19). Exhaustion from digging (#10, #21). Death from FP reaching 0.

**Tiwas equivalent:** DEC-015 (Reserved — environmental hazards explicitly listed). DEC-037 point (3) notes that "Global/systemic threats (Drowning, Suffocation, Extreme Temperature, Poison) apply direct HP/Conditions without a Location Index" — but this is a ruling about *how* such hazards interact with the location system, not a hazard-resolution mechanism.

**Readiness: Design-Stage Dependency.** The DEC-037 ruling acknowledges extreme temperature as a category and establishes that it bypasses Location Index, but no actual hazard-resolution mechanics (damage rates, resistance checks, condition thresholds) exist. The functional requirement (gradual resource depletion from environmental exposure) could potentially use Physical Energy expenditure, but no formal framework is defined.

---

### 4.12 Climbing and Physical Challenges

**Adventure usage:** Climbing rolls at various penalties (paragraphs #15, #35). DX-5 for unskilled climbing. Penalty for encumbrance. Fall damage on failure (1d-1, 2d-2).

**Tiwas equivalent:** No specific climbing subsystem. Would use the general Core Test Transaction (DEC-006) with a relevant skill (Body/Speed-based). Difficulty modifiers could use S-8 (DEC-063–DEC-066, named tiers with fixed additive Skill-side modifiers).

**Readiness: Playtestable with Adaptation.** A climbing check is a standard Core Test against a Body/Speed skill. Difficulty penalties map to S-8's Skill-side modifiers. Fall damage maps to Overflow or a GM-adjudicated HP loss. No new subsystem needed, but the GM must make ad-hoc rulings for difficulty grades and fall damage amounts.

---

### 4.13 Fear/Morale Mechanics

**Adventure usage:** Paragraph #2 requires a "WAIL roll, modified by Fearlessness." Ice Troll Will roll to determine retreat vs. fight-to-death at 0 HP (#17).

**Tiwas equivalent:** No specific fear/morale subsystem. Would use opposed contest (S-1) or a simple Core Test against a Mind attribute skill (e.g., Willpower, mpe-based). DEC-052 (HP = 0 forced incapacitation) covers the player side but not NPC morale.

**Readiness: Design-Stage Dependency.** The Core Test can resolve a fear check mechanically, but NPC morale/retreat behaviour has no framework. The Ice Troll's "Will roll at 0 HP to retreat vs. fight-to-death" requires a morale subsystem or GM ad-hoc ruling. Willpower (mpe) exists as a Tiwas skill, but its application to NPC behavioural decisions is undefined.

---

### 4.14 Healing and First Aid

**Adventure usage:** First Aid / Physician rolls to restore HP after combat (#21). Bandaging wounds. Splinting broken legs (#32).

**Tiwas equivalent:** DEC-071–DEC-074 (S-11 Rest/Healing). Healing requires an explicit Skill Test (full Core Test). Wound magnitude penalises healer's effective Skill. S-11 is treated as an Extended Test instance (S-9/S-10). Completion target at GM discretion.

**Readiness: Design-Stage Dependency.** S-11 is ruled but:
- Source handoff is **DRAFT pending Tiwa reconfirmation** (the DEC entries are operative, but the system has not been playtested).
- Depends on S-4 Wound system (which itself depends on S-3 Effect contents being enumerated).
- Depends on S-9/S-10 Extended Tests (ruled but untested).
- The interaction chain (S-3 → S-4 → S-9/S-10 → S-11) has never been exercised end-to-end.

---

### 4.15 Lockpicking

**Adventure usage:** Paragraph #18 — Lockpicking roll at +2 for old stone lock (or DX-3 unskilled). Failure allows retry via door-breaking.

**Tiwas equivalent:** Standard Core Test (DEC-006) against a relevant skill. Difficulty modifier via S-8 (DEC-063).

**Readiness: Playtestable Now.** A lockpicking check is a straightforward Core Test. The +2 bonus maps to an S-8 difficulty adjustment (easier tier). No special subsystem required.

---

### 4.16 Tracking

**Adventure usage:** Paragraph #27 — Tracking roll at +3 (or IQ-2 unskilled) to navigate an icy maze and follow tracks.

**Tiwas equivalent:** Standard Core Test against a Perception or Tracking skill. S-8 difficulty modifier.

**Readiness: Playtestable Now.** Same as lockpicking — a standard Core Test with a difficulty adjustment.

---

### 4.17 Tactical Map-Based Combat

**Adventure usage:** Paragraph #17 references a tactical map at the end of the adventure for the Ice Troll fight. Starting positions defined (bone pile, entrance hex, blood-stained snow hex for ambush). Grid-based movement and positioning.

**Tiwas equivalent:** No tactical combat map system. Movement Speed exists (DEC-004: floor((bsp + bss) / 15)) but no grid, range bands, or positioning rules.

**Readiness: Missing Subsystem.** No tactical combat infrastructure exists. The adventure's map-based encounter cannot be directly replicated. A Theatre-of-the-Mind approach could substitute, but the adventure explicitly assumes grid positioning.

---

### 4.18 Opposed Contests

**Adventure usage:** Implicit in combat (attack vs. defence), and potentially in social/interaction scenarios (though the adventure is light on these).

**Tiwas equivalent:** DEC-013 (S-1 Universal Opposed Contest). Fully locked, canonical. Includes Margin, Blackjack, and Hybrid Committed Quality modes. Failure/Failure repeats. Exact Quality tie repeats.

**Readiness: Playtestable Now.** S-1 is complete and locked. Any opposed interaction in the adventure maps directly to S-1.

---

## 5. Readiness Summary Matrix

| # | System | Adventure Usage | Tiwas Status | Readiness |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Core Resolution (d100 roll-under) | All checks | DEC-001, DEC-006 — Locked | **Playtestable Now** |
| 2 | Attribute System | DX, ST, IQ, Will, HT checks | DEC-003 — Locked (24-attribute) | **Playtestable with Adaptation** |
| 3 | Skill System | 15+ skills invoked | DEC-005 — Locked | **Playtestable Now** |
| 4 | Damage System | Multiple die expressions, damage types | DEC-007, DEC-023, DEC-032 — Partially ruled | **Design-Stage Dependency** |
| 5 | HP / Resource Pools | HP tracking, FP depletion | DEC-004 — Locked | **Playtestable with Adaptation** |
| 6 | Combat (Attack/Defence) | Full combat encounter | S-1 Locked; S-3/S-6 Ruled but untested | **Design-Stage Dependency** |
| 7 | Monster/NPC Stat Blocks | Ice Troll, Blood Man | DEC-076/077 — Path defined, no content | **Missing Subsystem** |
| 8 | Armour / DR | DR 2, DR 1, shield | S-5 Ruled, no Tag content | **Design-Stage Dependency** |
| 9 | Weapon/Equipment Stats | Mace, dagger, shield, rope | DEC-015 — Reserved | **Missing Subsystem** |
| 10 | Encumbrance | Climbing penalty | DEC-015 — Reserved | **Missing Subsystem** |
| 11 | Environmental Hazards (Cold) | FP loss, hypothermia | DEC-037 point (3) — acknowledged, no mechanics | **Design-Stage Dependency** |
| 12 | Climbing / Physical Challenges | Climb checks, fall damage | Core Test + S-8 (adaptable) | **Playtestable with Adaptation** |
| 13 | Fear / Morale | WAIL roll, troll retreat check | No subsystem; Willpower skill exists | **Design-Stage Dependency** |
| 14 | Healing / First Aid | HP restoration, wound treatment | S-11 Ruled, DRAFT source, untested | **Design-Stage Dependency** |
| 15 | Lockpicking | Lockpick roll with modifier | Standard Core Test + S-8 | **Playtestable Now** |
| 16 | Tracking | Tracking roll with modifier | Standard Core Test + S-8 | **Playtestable Now** |
| 17 | Tactical Map Combat | Grid positions, starting hexes | No subsystem | **Missing Subsystem** |
| 18 | Opposed Contests | Combat, implicit social | DEC-013 — Locked | **Playtestable Now** |

---

## 6. Readiness Distribution

| Category | Count | Systems |
|---|---|---|
| **Playtestable Now** | 6 | Core Resolution, Skill System, Lockpicking, Tracking, Opposed Contests, *(HP/Resource Pools partially)* |
| **Playtestable with Adaptation** | 3 | Attribute System, HP/Resource Pools, Climbing/Physical Challenges |
| **Design-Stage Dependency** | 6 | Damage System, Combat (Attack/Defence), Armour/DR, Environmental Hazards, Fear/Morale, Healing/First Aid |
| **Missing Subsystem** | 4 | Monster/NPC Stat Blocks, Weapon/Equipment Stats, Encumbrance, Tactical Map Combat |

---

## 7. Dependency Map — Blocking Chain

The following diagram shows which Tiwas subsystems must be completed before the adventure is fully playable. Arrows indicate "depends on."

```
FULL ADVENTURE PLAYABILITY
    │
    ├──► S-12 Creature Templates (MISSING)
    │       └──► Requires: DEC-041 anatomy (Ruled), playtesting path (DEC-077)
    │
    ├──► Equipment / Weapon Stats (MISSING — Reserved, DEC-015)
    │       └──► Blocks: weapon damage, item Tags, inventory
    │               └──► Blocks: S-5 Armour Tag vocabulary
    │
    ├──► Encumbrance (MISSING — Reserved, DEC-015)
    │       └──► Blocks: climbing penalties, movement
    │
    ├──► S-3 Gated-Tier Effect Contents (DESIGN-STAGE)
    │       └──► Blocks: S-4 Wound consequences, S-6 Defence interactions
    │               └──► Blocks: S-11 Healing (wound-magnitude penalty)
    │
    ├──► S-6 Active Defence (RULED, UNTESTED)
    │       └──► Depends on: S-3 Effect contents, S-5 Armour Tags
    │
    ├──► S-5 Armour Tag Content (RULED STRUCTURE, NO CONTENT)
    │       └──► Depends on: Equipment/Item Tag vocabulary
    │
    ├──► Environmental Hazard Mechanics (DESIGN-STAGE)
    │       └──► DEC-037 acknowledges category; no resolution framework
    │
    ├──► NPC Morale / Behavioural Mechanics (DESIGN-STAGE)
    │       └──► No subsystem; GM ad-hoc only
    │
    └──► Tactical Combat / Grid System (MISSING)
            └──► No subsystem; Theatre-of-Mind substitute possible
```

**Critical path for minimum viable playtest:**
1. Equipment/Weapon stats (even a minimal set for the adventure's specific items)
2. Creature stat blocks (Ice Troll, Blood Man — at least two templates)
3. A simplified combat resolution sequence (S-1 + S-3 base tier + ad-hoc damage)
4. Environmental hazard ruling (cold exposure → Physical Energy drain or HP loss)

---

## 8. Recommendations

### 8.1 What Can Be Playtested Now

The following adventure elements can be run under current Tiwas rules with minimal adaptation:

- **All non-combat skill checks** (lockpicking, tracking, climbing, survival, perception, merchant, first aid attempts) — standard Core Tests with S-8 difficulty modifiers.
- **The choose-your-path narrative structure** — no mechanical dependency.
- **Basic opposed contests** — S-1 is fully functional.
- **Resource depletion from exertion** — Physical Energy expenditure for strenuous activity.

### 8.2 What Requires GM Ad-Hoc Ruling

- **Damage amounts** — GM must assign HP loss for falls, environmental hazards, and monster attacks without a weapon-damage framework.
- **Monster behaviour** — GM must decide Ice Troll retreat/fight-to-death without a morale subsystem.
- **Equipment benefits** — GM must adjudicate shield/armour/weapon bonuses without item Tags.
- **Cold exposure** — GM must implement a resource-drain mechanic for the adventure's pervasive cold theme.

### 8.3 Priority Development Items for Full Playability

Ranked by blocking impact:

| Priority | Subsystem | Rationale |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Equipment / Item Tags (minimal vocabulary) | Blocks weapon damage, armour content, inventory decisions — central to the adventure |
| 2 | Creature Templates (S-12, at least 2) | Two combat encounters require statted opponents |
| 3 | S-3 Gated-Tier Effect Contents | Combat needs more than base-tier Inflict Injury to be meaningful |
| 4 | Environmental Hazard Framework | Cold exposure is a core adventure mechanic, not a sidebar |
| 5 | NPC Morale / Behavioural Rules | Ice Troll fight-to-death decision is a key encounter design element |
| 6 | Tactical Combat (or Theatre-of-Mind guidelines) | Adventure assumes grid; need either grid rules or explicit ToTM guidance |

### 8.4 Proposed Minimum Viable Playtest Scope

A **reduced-scope playtest** could exercise the following Tiwas systems using this adventure:

- Core Test Transaction (DEC-006) — every skill check
- S-1 Opposed Contest (DEC-013) — combat encounters (simplified)
- S-8 Difficulty grades (DEC-063–DEC-066) — environmental and situational modifiers
- Attribute/Skill mapping (DEC-003, DEC-005) — character conversion
- HP/Physical Energy resource management (DEC-004, DEC-007) — resource depletion

This would require GM ad-hoc rulings for damage, equipment, and monster stats, but would exercise Tiwas's core resolution engine extensively across diverse scenarios (exploration, combat, hazard, skill challenge).

---

## 9. Appendix: Adventure Mechanical Demand Heat Map
```
